# Computing A Well-Representative Summary of Conjunctive Query Results*

计算合取查询结果的代表性摘要*

PANKAJ K. AGARWAL, Department of Computer Science, Duke University, USA

潘卡吉·K·阿加瓦尔，美国杜克大学计算机科学系

ARYAN ESMAILPOUR, Department of Computer Science, University of Illinois Chicago, USA

阿里安·埃斯梅尔普尔，美国伊利诺伊大学芝加哥分校计算机科学系

XIAO HU, Cheriton School of Computer Science, University of Waterloo, Canada

胡晓，加拿大滑铁卢大学Cheriton计算机科学学院

STAVROS SINTOS, Department of Computer Science, University of Illinois Chicago, USA

斯塔夫罗斯·辛托斯，美国伊利诺伊大学芝加哥分校计算机科学系

JUN YANG, Department of Computer Science, Duke University, USA

杨军，美国杜克大学计算机科学系

Data summarization is a powerful approach to deal with large-scale data analytics, which has wide applications in web search, recommendation systems, approximate query processing, etc. It computes a small, compact summary that preserves vital properties of the original data. In this paper, we study the data summarization problem of conjunctive query results,i.e.,computing a $k$ -size subset of a conjunctive query output,for any given $k > 0$ ,that optimizes a certain objective. More specifically,we are interested in two commonly studied objectives: cohesion, which measures the maximum distance between a tuple in the query result tuples and its closest tuple in the summary ( $k$ -center clustering); and diversity,which measures the pairwise distances between the summary items. A simple approach that computes the entire query output and then applies existing algorithms on top of these materialized tuples suffers from high computational complexity because the query output can be large,e.g.,for a relational database of $N$ tuples,the number of result tuples can be ${N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$ . We propose $O\left( 1\right)$ -approximation algorithms that compute well-representative summaries of size $k$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$ ,or even $\widetilde{O}\left( {N + {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$ in some cases, ${}^{1}$ without computing all result tuples. We also propose the first efficient $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -approximation algorithm for the $k$ -center clustering problem over relational data. Our main idea is to formulate a few oracles that enable us to access specific query result tuples with certain properties, to show how these oracles can be implemented efficiently, and to compute desired summaries with few invocations of these oracles.

数据摘要技术是处理大规模数据分析的强大方法，在网页搜索、推荐系统、近似查询处理等领域有广泛应用。该方法通过计算原始数据的小型紧凑摘要来保留其关键特性。本文研究合取查询结果的数据摘要问题，即针对任意给定$k > 0$，从合取查询输出中计算$k$大小的子集以优化特定目标。我们重点关注两个常见目标：内聚性（衡量查询结果元组与摘要中最近元组之间的最大距离，即$k$-中心聚类）和多样性（衡量摘要项之间的成对距离）。传统方法需先计算完整查询结果再应用现有算法，但由于查询结果可能非常庞大（例如$N$元组的关系数据库可能产生${N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$个结果元组），计算复杂度极高。我们提出$O\left( 1\right)$近似算法，可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$时间（某些情况下甚至$\widetilde{O}\left( {N + {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$）内计算$k$大小的代表性摘要，且${}^{1}$无需计算所有结果元组。我们还首次提出针对关系数据$k$-中心聚类问题的高效$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$近似算法。核心思路是构建若干能访问特定属性查询结果元组的预言机，展示其高效实现方法，并通过少量预言机调用计算理想摘要。

## CCS Concepts: - Theory of computation $\rightarrow$ Data structures and algorithms for data management.

## CCS概念：- 计算理论$\rightarrow$ 数据管理的数据结构与算法

Additional Key Words and Phrases: relational data, conjunctive queries, diversity, coresets, oracles

附加关键词：关系数据、合取查询、多样性、核心集、预言机

## 1 Introduction

## 1 引言

Data summarization is a potent strategy in large-scale data analytics, offering a means to compute a compact yet comprehensive dataset that preserves vital properties of the original data. A multitude of data summarization techniques have been developed for diverse applications, encompassing sampling [12, 23, 40, 53], histograms [47, 48], wavelet-based synopses [29, 39], sketching [5, 27, 28], coresets [6], and more [29]. The objectives on the quality of the data summaries strongly depend on the downstream application scenarios. Viewing query result tuples as points in a multi-dimensional space, two common objectives are cohesion, which measures the maximum distance between a result tuple and its closest tuple in the summary, and diversity, which measures the minimum or average pairwise distance between tuples in the summary.

数据摘要（Data summarization）是大规模数据分析中的一项有效策略，它能够计算出一个紧凑而全面的数据集，同时保留原始数据的关键特性。目前已开发出多种数据摘要技术，涵盖采样[12,23,40,53]、直方图[47,48]、基于小波的概要[29,39]、草图[5,27,28]、核心集[6]等方法[29]。数据摘要的质量目标高度依赖于下游应用场景。将查询结果元组视为多维空间中的点时，两个常见目标是内聚性（测量结果元组与摘要中最近元组之间的最大距离）和多样性（测量摘要中元组间的最小或平均成对距离）。

Most past data-summarization algorithms assume they are directly given the data to summarize. However, one often desires to summarize the output of a query, especially when the query output is large. In such applications, the simple approach of computing all query result tuples and then applying a known algorithm is ineffective because of its high computational cost when the query output size is large (which is why we want to compute a summary in the first place). There is some recent work on computing summaries of query results [3, 11, 27, 57], but this line of work has focused on simple queries, such as range queries, where data resides in a single table. In real-world scenarios, more than 70% of the current data sets are relational [1], where data is stored in multiple tables, and the desired data is obtained by performing conjunctive queries - the combination of select, project, and join queries - on these tables. Note that the size of the output of a conjunctive query can be polynomially larger than the size of the tables. Furthermore, user queries may have very different local selection predicates. Hence, the challenging question is constructing a well-representative summary for the output of a given conjunctive query without computing and materializing its entire output. While there are some recent results on clustering in relational data $\left\lbrack  {{31},{54},{55}}\right\rbrack$ ,there is no result on efficiently computing a well-representative summary in relational data. Hence, in this paper, we take on this challenging question and investigate how to construct well-representative summaries for conjunctive query results efficiently.

过去大多数数据摘要算法都假设直接获得了待摘要的数据。然而人们常常需要对查询输出进行摘要，尤其是当查询结果庞大时。在此类应用中，先计算所有查询结果元组再应用现有算法的简单方法效率低下——因为当查询输出规模较大时计算成本过高（这正是我们最初需要计算摘要的原因）。近期虽有关于查询结果摘要的研究[3,11,27,57]，但这些工作仅针对范围查询等单表简单查询。现实场景中超过70%的现有数据集是关系型的[1]，数据存储在多个表中，需要通过连接查询（即选择、投影和连接操作的组合）获取目标数据。需注意连接查询的输出规模可能是指数级大于表规模的。此外用户查询可能包含差异巨大的本地选择谓词。因此核心挑战在于：如何在不计算和物化完整输出的情况下，为给定连接查询的结果构建具有良好代表性的摘要。尽管近期有关关系型数据聚类的研究$\left\lbrack  {{31},{54},{55}}\right\rbrack$，但尚未出现高效计算关系型数据优质摘要的成果。为此，本文针对这一难题展开研究，探索如何高效构建连接查询结果的代表性摘要。

### 1.1 Problem Definition

### 1.1 问题定义

Conjunctive query. Let $\mathbf{R}$ denote a database schema and $\mathbf{A}$ the set of all attributes. $\mathbf{R}$ consists of a set of $m$ relations $\left\{  {{R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m}}\right\}$ ,where each relation ${R}_{i}$ has a subset of attributes ${\mathbf{A}}_{i} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ ,satisfying $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{\mathbf{A}}_{i} = \mathbf{A}$ . Let $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ denote the domain of attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ . For the simplicity of exposition, we assume that all attributes have the domain $\mathbb{R}$ of reals,though our results can be generalized to other domains. A database instance $\mathrm{I}$ consists of the set $\left\{  {R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}\right\}$ of relational instances,where each ${R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}$ is a set of tuples over the domain ${\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right| }$ . Let $t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right| }$ denote a tuple in ${R}_{i}^{\mathbf{I}}$ : for each attribute $A \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$ , we use $t.A$ to denote $t$ ’s value for attribute $A$ ; for each subset of attributes $X \subseteq  {\mathrm{A}}_{i}$ ,we use $t.X$ to denote $t$ ’s projection onto attributes in $X$ . When the context is clear,we will drop the superscript $\mathrm{I}$ and simply refer to relation instance ${R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}$ as relation ${R}_{i}$ . We consider conjunctive queries (CQs):

合取查询。设$\mathbf{R}$表示数据库模式，$\mathbf{A}$表示所有属性的集合。$\mathbf{R}$由一组$m$关系$\left\{  {{R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m}}\right\}$组成，其中每个关系${R}_{i}$拥有属性子集${\mathbf{A}}_{i} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$，且满足$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{\mathbf{A}}_{i} = \mathbf{A}$。设$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$表示属性$A \in  \mathbf{A}$的域。为简化表述，我们假定所有属性的域均为实数集$\mathbb{R}$，但我们的结果可推广至其他域。数据库实例$\mathrm{I}$由关系实例集$\left\{  {R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}\right\}$构成，其中每个${R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}$是域${\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right| }$上的元组集合。设$t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right| }$表示${R}_{i}^{\mathbf{I}}$中的元组：对于每个属性$A \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$，用$t.A$表示$t$在属性$A$上的值；对于每个属性子集$X \subseteq  {\mathrm{A}}_{i}$，用$t.X$表示$t$在$X$属性上的投影。当上下文明确时，我们将省略上标$\mathrm{I}$，直接将关系实例${R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}$称为关系${R}_{i}$。我们考虑如下合取查询(CQs)：

$$
Q \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}\left( {\left( {{\sigma }_{{\mathrm{p}}_{1}}{R}_{1}}\right)  \bowtie  \left( {{\sigma }_{{\mathrm{p}}_{2}}{R}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  \left( {{\sigma }_{{\mathrm{p}}_{m}}{R}_{m}}\right) }\right) , \tag{1}
$$

where $\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ defines the set of output attributes,each ${\mathbf{p}}_{i}$ is a Boolean predicate over ${\mathbf{A}}_{i}$ ,and $\pi ,\sigma , \bowtie$ are relational projection, selection, and natural join operators. By renaming the attributes, we also allow self-joins in $Q$ ,i.e.,the same relation can be joined multiple times in $Q$ . Let $d = \left| \mathbf{y}\right|$ . The output of $Q$ over database instance $\mathbf{I}$ is defined as

其中$\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$定义输出属性集，每个${\mathbf{p}}_{i}$是${\mathbf{A}}_{i}$上的布尔谓词，$\pi ,\sigma , \bowtie$分别为关系投影、选择和自然连接运算符。通过属性重命名，我们允许$Q$中存在自连接，即同一关系可在$Q$中多次连接。设$d = \left| \mathbf{y}\right|$。$Q$在数据库实例$\mathbf{I}$上的输出定义为

$$
\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)  = \left\{  {{t}^{\prime } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}\mid \exists t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{A}\right| } : t.\mathbf{y} = {t}^{\prime } \land  \left( {\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack   : t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i} \in  {R}_{i}^{\mathbf{I}} \land  {\mathbf{p}}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\right) }\right) }\right\}  .
$$

Query $Q$ filters relation ${R}_{i}$ by the Boolean predicate ${\mathbf{p}}_{i}$ ,joins filtered tuples (one from each relation) sharing the same values on common attributes, and then projects the resulting tuples onto y. Each tuple in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ is essentially a point in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ .

查询$Q$通过布尔谓词${\mathbf{p}}_{i}$过滤关系${R}_{i}$，将过滤后的元组（来自各关系）在共有属性上等值连接，最后将结果元组投影至y。$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的每个元组本质上是${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中的一个点。

We say a CQ is full if $\mathbf{y} = \mathbf{A}$ (i.e., ${\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}$ is identity function). A full CQ is also called a join query. In this paper we mainly focus on acyclic CQs. Recall that a CQ is acyclic [16,37] if there exists a tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,called a join tree of $Q$ ,where 1) the nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ are ${R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m};2$ ) for each attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ ,the set of nodes whose attributes contain $A$ form an edge-connected subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ . For simplicity,in all cases we assume all dangling tuples are removed from ${\mathrm{I}}^{2}$

若$\mathbf{y} = \mathbf{A}$（即${\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}$为恒等函数），则称该合取查询为全查询。全查询也称为连接查询。本文主要研究无环合取查询。根据文献[16,37]，若存在树结构$\mathcal{T}$（称为$Q$的连接树）满足：1)树节点为${R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m};2$；2)对每个属性$A \in  \mathbf{A}$，包含$A$的节点集构成$\mathcal{T}$的边连通子树。为简化处理，所有情形均假定已从${\mathrm{I}}^{2}$移除悬空元组

Well-representative summaries for CQs. Given a CQ $Q$ of the form (1),a database I,and a positive integer $k$ ,we refer to a subset $S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ of $k$ distinct tuples from $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ as a $k$ -summary of $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . We use two different (though related) objectives to measure the quality of a summary. Let $\phi  : {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{y}\right| } \times  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{y}\right| } \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ \geq  0}$ be a distance function.

针对CQ的典型代表性摘要。给定形式(1)的CQ $Q$、数据库I和正整数$k$，我们将从$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中选取的$k$个不同元组子集$S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$称为$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$k$-摘要。采用两种不同（但相关）的目标函数来衡量摘要质量。设$\phi  : {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{y}\right| } \times  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{y}\right| } \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ \geq  0}$为距离函数。

- The cohesion of $S$ is defined using both $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and $S$ :

- $S$的内聚性通过$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$和$S$共同定义：

$$
\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{{t}_{2} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{{t}_{1} \in  S}}\phi \left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right) . \tag{2}
$$

Intuitively, cohesiveness ensures that every result tuple is close to some tuple in the summary. For any $k > 0$ ,let ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ denote the optimal cohesion of any $k$ -summary of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,i.e., ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\left| S\right|  = k}}\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . A k -summary ${S}^{ * }$ is called an optimally cohesive $k$ -summary if

直观而言，内聚性确保每个结果元组都与摘要中的某个元组相近。对于任意$k > 0$，令${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$表示$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$k$-摘要的最优内聚性，即${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\left| S\right|  = k}}\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。若满足条件，则称${S}^{ * }$为最优内聚性的$k$-摘要

$\rho \left( {{S}^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  = {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . For a parameter $\alpha  > 1$ ,a $k$ -summary $S$ is $\alpha$ -cohesive if $\rho \left( S\right)  \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . We note that the definition of cohesion is identical to the definition of the cost in the $k$ -center clustering problem. Hence,an $\alpha$ -cohesive $k$ -summary $S$ is also an $\alpha$ -approximation of $k$ -center clustering of $\mathbf{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ (relational $k$ -center problem).

$\rho \left( {{S}^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  = {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。给定参数$\alpha  > 1$，当$\rho \left( S\right)  \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$时，称$S$为$\alpha$-内聚的$k$-摘要。需注意该内聚性定义与$k$-中心聚类问题中的成本定义相同。因此，$\alpha$-内聚的$k$-摘要$S$同时也是$\mathbf{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$（关系型$k$-中心问题）的$k$-中心聚类的$\alpha$-近似解。

- The diversity of a set $S \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{y}\right| }$ ,has two variants:

- 集合$S \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| \mathbf{y}\right| }$的多样性具有两种变体：

$$
\text{Sum-diversity:}\delta \left( S\right)  = \frac{1}{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x,y \in  S \times  S}}\phi \left( {x,y}\right) \text{;} \tag{3}
$$

$$
\text{Min-diversity:}\;\delta \left( S\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{x,y \in  S \times  S : x \neq  y}}\phi \left( {x,y}\right) \text{.} \tag{4}
$$

Diversity seeks to ensure that summary tuples are far away from each other. For any $k > 0$ ,let ${\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ denote the optimal diversity of any $k$ -summary of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,i.e., ${\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\left| S\right|  = k}}\delta \left( S\right)$ .

多样性旨在确保摘要元组间彼此远离。对于任意$k > 0$，令${\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$表示$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$k$-摘要的最优多样性，即${\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\left| S\right|  = k}}\delta \left( S\right)$。

A $k$ -summary ${S}^{ * }$ is called an optimally diverse $k$ -summary if $\delta \left( {S}^{ * }\right)  = {\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Given a parameter $\alpha  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,a $k$ -summary $S$ is $\alpha$ -diverse if $\delta \left( S\right)  \geq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ .

若满足条件$\delta \left( {S}^{ * }\right)  = {\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$，则称$k$摘要${S}^{ * }$为最优多样化$k$摘要。给定参数$\alpha  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，当$\delta \left( S\right)  \geq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mu }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$时，$k$摘要$S$具有$\alpha$多样性。

Our goal is to compute efficiently well-representative summaries for an input $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ and database instance I. We are interested in the data complexity: i.e.,the query size $m$ is a constant,and the complexity of our algorithms is measured by the input size $N = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\left| {R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}\right|$ ,and the output size $k$ .

我们的目标是针对输入$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$和数据库实例I高效计算具有代表性的摘要。我们关注数据复杂度：即查询规模$m$为常数时，算法复杂度由输入规模$N = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\left| {R}_{i}^{\mathrm{I}}\right|$和输出规模$k$衡量。

### 1.2 Related Work

### 1.2 相关工作

Summaries of a given data set. Computing various summaries for a set $P$ of data points has been extensively studied in the literature under different objectives. A summary that maximizes the sum-diversity (3) is known as remote-clique or max-sum problem [59], and that maximizes the min-diversity (4) is known as remote-edge or max-min problem [64],which is NP-hard. ${}^{3}$ For the max-sum problem,there are efficient $\frac{1}{2}$ -diverse algorithms that work for any distance function [17, 18, 59]. Better algorithms are proposed for the Euclidean distance, either with better approximation factors [19-21] or with better time complexity [11]. For the max-min problem, Tamir [64] showed that a greedy algorithm returns a $\frac{1}{2}$ -approximation for any metric. A faster algorithm [11] is also known for the max-min problem the under Euclidean metric. Computing a summary of $P$ that minimize cohesion, ${}^{4}$ known as the $k$ -center problem,is NP-hard. ${}^{5}$ The well-known Gonzalez’s algorithm [41] returns a 2-cohesive $k$ -summary of a set $P$ in $O\left( {k \cdot  \left| P\right| }\right)$ time under any metric. Its running time was improved to $O\left( {\left| P\right| \log k}\right)$ in [38] (see also [44]). All the problems also have been studied under fairness constraints [2, 4, 18, 49, 51, 52, 56].

数据集摘要。文献中针对数据点集$P$的不同目标已广泛研究各类摘要计算。最大化总和多样性(3)的摘要称为远程团或最大和问题[59]，最大化最小多样性(4)的称为远程边或最大最小问题[64]，后者属于NP难问题。${}^{3}$对于最大和问题，存在适用于任意距离函数的高效$\frac{1}{2}$多样性算法[17,18,59]。针对欧氏距离提出了更优算法，或具有更好近似因子[19-21]，或具备更优时间复杂度[11]。对于最大最小问题，Tamir[64]证明贪心算法可返回任意度量下的$\frac{1}{2}$近似解。欧氏度量下的更快算法[11]也已提出。最小化内聚度的$P$摘要计算（即$k$中心问题）属于NP难问题。${}^{5}$著名的Gonzalez算法[41]可在$O\left( {k \cdot  \left| P\right| }\right)$时间内返回任意度量下2-内聚的$k$摘要，其时间复杂度在[38]中改进至$O\left( {\left| P\right| \log k}\right)$（另见[44]）。这些问题在公平性约束下也有研究[2,4,18,49,51,52,56]。

---

${}^{2}$悬空元组指不参与底层连接查询任何结果的元组，对于无环连接查询可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内完成检测。详见附录A。

---

Summaries of selection results. There is some work on computing the summaries of range query outputs of a set of points $P$ . A near-linear-size index exists [3,57] that,given any query rectangle $\psi$ ,computes a $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -cohesive summary $k$ -summary of $P \cap  \psi$ in $O\left( {k \cdot  \text{polylog}\left( \left| P\right| \right) }\right)$ time. Subsequently,a similar index was shown to generate $O\left( 1\right)$ -diverse summaries for $P \cap  \psi$ . Additionally, summaries have been extensively studied for statistical queries, such as sampling, sketching, frequent moments, and embedding. We refer interested readers to [30] for details.

选择结果摘要。关于点集$P$范围查询输出的摘要计算已有若干研究。[3,57]提出了一种近线性规模的索引，可在给定查询矩形$\psi$时，于$O\left( {k \cdot  \text{polylog}\left( \left| P\right| \right) }\right)$时间内生成$P \cap  \psi$的$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$-内聚摘要$k$。后续研究表明该索引还能生成$P \cap  \psi$的$O\left( 1\right)$多样性摘要。此外，针对统计查询（如采样、素描、频繁矩和嵌入）的摘要研究也相当深入，详见[30]。

Summaries of join results. Computing summaries of the results for a join query is useful to answer analytical queries while providing provably accuracy guarantees, such as sampling [24, 26, 34, 50, ${66},{67}\rbrack$ ,factorization [58] and witness [46]. Recently,the $k$ -means and $k$ -median clustering problems over relational data have been studied $\left\lbrack  {{31},{36},{55}}\right\rbrack$ . The coreset for empirical risk minimization problems over relational data [25] has also been considered. However, the time complexity of their algorithm for constructing an $\varepsilon$ -coreset depends on the diameter of the query results. Merkl et al. [54] studied the hardness of diversity problems over relational data under the Hamming metric. Under data complexity, they give an expensive algorithm (computing all query results) to construct an $O\left( {k}^{m}\right)$ (exact) coreset. Arenas et al. [13],also studied the hardness of diversity problems under the Hamming metric and ultrametrics. Furthermore, they propose polynomial time algorithms for some diversity problems under ultrametrics (Euclidean and Hamming metrics are not ultrametrics).

连接查询结果的摘要计算。对连接查询结果进行摘要计算，能在提供可证明精度保证（如采样[24, 26, 34, 50, ${66},{67}\rbrack$]、因子分解[58]和见证[46]）的同时，有效回答分析性查询。近年来，针对关系数据的$k$-均值与$k$-中位数聚类问题已有研究$\left\lbrack  {{31},{36},{55}}\right\rbrack$。关系数据上经验风险最小化问题的核心集[25]也得到探讨。然而，其构建$\varepsilon$-核心集算法的时间复杂度取决于查询结果的直径。Merkl等人[54]研究了汉明度量下关系数据多样性问题的计算难度，在数据复杂度层面给出了构建$O\left( {k}^{m}\right)$（精确）核心集的昂贵算法（需计算全部查询结果）。Arenas等人[13]同样研究了汉明度量与超度量下的多样性问题难度，并针对超度量（欧氏与汉明度量不属于超度量）下的部分多样性问题提出了多项式时间算法。

### 1.3 Our Contributions

### 1.3 我们的贡献

For a CQ $Q$ with $d$ output attributes,a database I of input size $N$ ,and a parameter $k$ ,we propose several $O\left( 1\right)$ -approximation algorithms that compute cohesive and diverse $k$ -summaries for $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ in $\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$ or $\widetilde{O}\left( {N + {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$ time,under the Euclidean or Hamming metric. We include all the $\log N$ factors in the analyses and theorems in the next sections. All our results for acyclic join queries are shown in Table 1, and the extended results to cyclic join queries and even join-project queries are discussed in Section 6. In the next sections we use the notation $\phi \left( {\cdot , \cdot  }\right)$ for the Euclidean distance and ${\phi }_{H}\left( {\cdot , \cdot  }\right)$ for the Hamming distance. For two tuples $p,q \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,let $\phi \left( {p,q}\right)  = {\left( \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}}}{\left( p.{A}_{j} - q.{A}_{j}\right) }^{2}\right) }^{1/2}$ and ${\phi }_{H}\left( {p,q}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}}}\mathbb{1}\left( {p.{A}_{j} \neq  q.{A}_{j}}\right)$ ,where1is the indicator function.

对于具有$d$个输出属性的CQ $Q$、输入规模为$N$的数据库I及参数$k$，我们提出若干$O\left( 1\right)$-近似算法，可在欧氏或汉明度量下以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$或$\widetilde{O}\left( {N + {k}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\right)$时间复杂度为$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$计算内聚且多样的$k$-摘要。后续章节的分析与定理中包含了所有$\log N$因子。我们对无环连接查询的全部结果总结于表1，第6节讨论了将结果扩展至循环连接查询乃至连接-投影查询的情况。后续章节采用$\phi \left( {\cdot , \cdot  }\right)$表示欧氏距离，${\phi }_{H}\left( {\cdot , \cdot  }\right)$表示汉明距离。对于两个元组$p,q \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，定义$\phi \left( {p,q}\right)  = {\left( \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}}}{\left( p.{A}_{j} - q.{A}_{j}\right) }^{2}\right) }^{1/2}$与${\phi }_{H}\left( {p,q}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}}}\mathbb{1}\left( {p.{A}_{j} \neq  q.{A}_{j}}\right)$，其中1为指示函数。

- Cohesive summary (Section 3):

- 内聚摘要（第3节）：

- Euclidean metric (Section 3.1). We design an algorithm to construct a $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary in $\widetilde{O}\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N,{k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}   + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time. This result also leads to the first efficient $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -approximation algorithm for the relational $k$ -center clustering problem. The best previously known algorithm for the relational $k$ -centering problem has either an additive approximation factor that depends on the diameter of $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ or a relative approximation factor that depends exponentially on $d$ [25]. In order to derive this result,we first construct two $O\left( 1\right)$ - cohesive summaries: (i) a tree-based algorithm that runs in $O\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{2}}\right)$ space,

- 欧几里得度量（第3.1节）。我们设计了一种算法，可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N,{k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}   + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$时间内构建$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$内聚的$k$摘要。该成果同时催生了首个针对关系型$k$中心聚类问题的高效$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$近似算法。此前最先进的关系型$k$中心化算法要么具有取决于$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$直径的加法近似因子，要么具有与$d$呈指数关系的相对近似因子[25]。为实现这一突破，我们首先构建了两个$O\left( 1\right)$内聚摘要：(i)基于树的算法，耗时$O\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$并使用$O\left( {N + {k}^{2}}\right)$存储空间。

but whose approximation depends on the number of relations in the join query; (ii) a geometry-based 6-approximation algorithm that runs in $O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$ space. We then combine the $O\left( 1\right)$ -approximation algorithms with a grid-based construction to derive a $\varepsilon$ -coreset (formally defined in Section 3.1.3) of $O\left( {{\varepsilon }^{-d}k}\right)$ centers such that every result tuple from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ is "near" enough to some center from the coreset. The $\varepsilon$ -coreset leads to the construction of a $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary in $\widetilde{O}\left( {\min \left\{  {N{k}^{2},{k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}   + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time.

但其近似度取决于连接查询中的关系数量；(ii) 基于几何的6-近似算法，该算法在$O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$时间内运行并占用$O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$空间。随后我们将$O\left( 1\right)$-近似算法与基于网格的构造相结合，推导出包含$O\left( {{\varepsilon }^{-d}k}\right)$个中心的$\varepsilon$-核心集（3.1.3节正式定义），使得来自$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的每个结果元组都与核心集中的某个中心足够"接近"。该$\varepsilon$-核心集可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {\min \left\{  {N{k}^{2},{k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}   + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$时间内构建出$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$-内聚$k$-摘要。

- Hamming metric (Section 3.2). Using an iterative approach that implicitly excludes tuples close to the selected tuples in the summary, we present an algorithm that constructs a 2-cohesive $k$ -summary in $O\left( {N \cdot  {k}^{d}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$ space.

- 汉明度量（3.2节）。通过采用隐式排除摘要中已选元组邻近元组的迭代方法，我们提出一种算法，可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {k}^{d}}\right)$时间内利用$O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$空间构建2-内聚$k$-摘要。

- Min-Diverse summary (Section 4):

- 最小多样性摘要（第4节）：

- Euclidean metric (Section 4.1). We first show that any cohesive coreset is also a min-diverse coreset. Hence, using the ideas for constructing cohesive summaries, we design an algorithm to construct a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -min-diverse $k$ -summary in $\widetilde{O}\left( {\min \left\{  {N{k}^{2},{k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}   + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time.

- 欧氏度量（4.1节）。我们首先证明任何内聚核心集同时也是最小多样性核心集。因此，借鉴构建内聚摘要的思路，我们设计出能在$\widetilde{O}\left( {\min \left\{  {N{k}^{2},{k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}   + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$时间内构建$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$-最小多样性$k$-摘要的算法。

- Hamming metric (Section 4.2). Using ideas for constructing cohesive summaries, we design an algorithm to construct a $\frac{1}{2}$ -min-diverse summary in $O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$ space. - Sum-Diverse summary (Section 5):

- 汉明度量（4.2节）。运用构建内聚摘要的思想，我们开发出可在$O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$空间构建$\frac{1}{2}$-最小多样性摘要的算法。- 求和多样性摘要（第5节）：

- Euclidean metric (Section 5.1). We present a geometric approach using the notion of $\varepsilon$ -net that can construct an $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -diverse $k$ -summary in $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left( {N + k}\right) {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k{\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$ space.

- 欧氏度量（5.1节）。我们提出基于$\varepsilon$-网概念的几何方法，可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left( {N + k}\right) {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$时间内利用$O\left( {N + k{\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$空间构建$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$-多样性$k$-摘要。

- Hamming metric (Section 5.2). We propose two algorithms to construct sum-diverse summaries. The first one,a local search algorithm,constructs a $\left( {1 - 2/k}\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary in $\widetilde{O}\left( {N{k}^{2} + {k}^{3}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k}\right)$ space. The second one,a greedy algorithm,constructs a $\frac{1}{2}$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary in $\widetilde{O}\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{2}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k}\right)$ space.

- 汉明度量（Hamming metric，见第5.2节）。我们提出两种构建总和多样性摘要的算法：第一种是局部搜索算法，可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N{k}^{2} + {k}^{3}}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + k}\right)$空间构建$\left( {1 - 2/k}\right)$-总和多样性$k$-摘要；第二种是贪心算法，可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{2}}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + k}\right)$空间构建$\frac{1}{2}$-总和多样性$k$-摘要。

Main ideas. As mentioned, to summarize the output of a CQ, one could compute all result tuples first and then directly apply existing algorithms developed for computing summaries for a given dataset $\left\lbrack  {{41},{59},{64}}\right\rbrack$ . However,materializing the entire query output is expensive. Instead, our approach is to formulate appropriate oracles that enable us to access some specific query result tuples with certain properties-including nearest neighbor,farthest neighbor,top- $k$ ,and rectangular lookup-and show how to modify existing algorithms or design new algorithms using as few invocations of these oracles as possible. One key insight is that our computation can be modeled as ranked enumeration of query answers. Conceptually,given a CQ $Q$ ,a set of weight functions defined on attributes,and a database I,ranked enumeration returns $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ in ascending (or descending) order with respect to their weights, one at a time, with a bounded delay between consecutive answers. We carefully design weight functions using query and data to apply ranked enumeration in our settings. We also push selections down as far as possible. Specifically, given a set of selection predicates, each on an individual attribute, we simply push the selection predicates down to the base tables first and enumerate all result tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ satisfying the selection predicates with a bounded delay between consecutive answers.

核心思路。如前所述，为汇总CQ输出结果，可先计算所有结果元组再直接应用现有摘要生成算法$\left\lbrack  {{41},{59},{64}}\right\rbrack$。但物化整个查询输出代价高昂。我们采用的方法是构建特定预言机（oracle），通过这些预言机访问具有特定属性（包括最近邻、最远邻、前$k$项及矩形查找）的查询结果元组，并展示如何以最少调用次数改造现有算法或设计新算法。关键突破在于将计算过程建模为查询答案的排序枚举——给定CQ$Q$、属性权重函数集和数据库I，排序枚举会按权重升序（或降序）逐个返回$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$结果，且保证相邻结果间的延迟有界。我们巧妙设计查询与数据相关的权重函数，并将选择谓词尽可能下推至基表，实现带约束条件的延迟有界枚举。

## 2 Relational Oracles

## 2 关系型预言机

We show some relational oracles that will be commonly used throughout the paper.

本节展示本文通用的关系型预言机。

Ranked enumeration. Let ${w}_{i} : {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathrm{A}}_{i}\right| } \rightarrow  \mathbb{R}$ be a weight function,which takes as input a tuple $t \in  {R}_{i}$ and outputs a real number. Let $\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$ be a set of weight functions. For a CQ $\mathcal{Q}$ ,a database $\mathbf{I}$ ,and a pair of results ${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ ,we say ${t}_{1}{ \leq  }_{\overrightarrow{w}}{t}_{2}$ if $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{1}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\right)  \leq$ $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{2}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\right)$ .

排序枚举。设${w}_{i} : {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathrm{A}}_{i}\right| } \rightarrow  \mathbb{R}$为权重函数（输入元组$t \in  {R}_{i}$输出实数值），$\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$为权重函数集。对于CQ$\mathcal{Q}$、数据库$\mathbf{I}$及结果对${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，当$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{1}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\right)  \leq$$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{2}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\right)$时称${t}_{1}{ \leq  }_{\overrightarrow{w}}{t}_{2}$。

LEMMA 2.1 ([33]). For an acyclic join $Q$ ,a database $\mathbf{I}$ ,and a set of weight functions $\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$ , an index of size $O\left( N\right)$ can be constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time,such that given any value $k \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$ ,the top- $k$ results of $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be enumerated in ascending or descending order with respect to $\overrightarrow{w}$ within $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ delay using $O\left( k\right)$ additional space.

引理2.1（文献[33]）。对于无环连接$Q$、数据库$\mathbf{I}$和权重函数集$\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$，可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构建大小为$O\left( N\right)$的索引，使得给定任意值$k \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$时，能使用$O\left( k\right)$额外空间在$O\left( {\log N}\right)$延迟内按$\overrightarrow{w}$升序或降序枚举$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的前$k$个结果。

By exploiting the variety of weight functions supported by ranked enumeration, we are able to define the Euclidean-based oracles and the top- $k$ oracle. For simplicity,let ${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i} = {\mathbf{A}}_{i} - \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{j < i}}{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\right)$ be the set of active attributes for ${R}_{i}$ ,i.e.,the attributes in ${R}_{i}$ that do not appear in a relation ${R}_{j}$ for $j < i$ .

通过利用排序枚举支持的多种权重函数，我们能够定义基于欧氏距离的预言机（oracle）和前$k$预言机。简言之，令${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i} = {\mathbf{A}}_{i} - \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{j < i}}{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\right)$表示${R}_{i}$的活跃属性集，即${R}_{i}$中未出现在$j < i$的关系${R}_{j}$内的属性。

Euclidean-based oracles. Let $\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ be a tuple. The nearest (resp. farthest) neighbor oracle finds a tuple $t \in  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ that is closest to $\theta$ under the Euclidean metric. For each relation ${R}_{i}$ and for a tuple $p \in  {R}_{i}$ ,we define ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}}{\left( p \cdot  {A}_{j} - \theta  \cdot  {A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$ . It is easy to see for any query result $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ ,

基于欧氏距离的预言机。设$\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$为元组。最近邻（或最远邻）预言机可找到欧氏度量下距$\theta$最近（或最远）的元组$t \in  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$。对于每个关系${R}_{i}$和元组$p \in  {R}_{i}$，我们定义${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}}{\left( p \cdot  {A}_{j} - \theta  \cdot  {A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$。易见对于任意查询结果$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathrm{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}}{\left( t \cdot  {A}_{j} - \theta  \cdot  {A}_{j}\right) }^{2} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  \mathrm{A}}}{\left( t \cdot  A - \theta  \cdot  A\right) }^{2} = {\phi }^{2}\left( {\theta ,t}\right) ,
$$

thanks to the decomposability of the squared Euclidean distance. The square (and square root) function is increasing for non-negative values, so the squared Euclidean distance preserves the ordering of Euclidean distance.

这归功于平方欧氏距离的可分解性。平方（及平方根）函数在非负值上单调递增，故平方欧氏距离保持了欧氏距离的序关系。

Top- $k$ oracle. Let $u = \left\langle  {{u}_{1},{u}_{2},\ldots ,{u}_{d}}\right\rangle   \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ be a vector. The top- $k$ oracle finds the $k$ tuples from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ with the largest inner product with $u$ . For each relation ${R}_{i}$ and for any $p \in  {R}_{i}$ ,we define ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{A}}_{i}}}\left( {p{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$ . It is easy to see that for any query result $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,

前$k$预言机。设$u = \left\langle  {{u}_{1},{u}_{2},\ldots ,{u}_{d}}\right\rangle   \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$为向量。前$k$预言机从$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中找出与$u$内积最大的$k$个元组。对于每个关系${R}_{i}$和任意$p \in  {R}_{i}$，我们定义${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{A}}_{i}}}\left( {p{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$。显然对于任意查询结果$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t \cdot  {\mathrm{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}}\left( {t \cdot  {A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j} = \langle t,u\rangle .
$$

LEMMA 2.2. Given an acyclic join $Q$ ,a database instance $\mathbf{I}$ with input size $N$ ,and a tuple $\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ , the nearest (resp. farthest) neighbor of $\theta$ in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,under the Euclidean metric,can be computed in $O\left( N\right)$ time. Each tuple in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be enumerated in ascending or descending order with respect to its distance from $\theta$ within $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ delay. Furthermore,given a vector $u \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,the $k$ tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ with the highest inner product with $u$ can be computed in $O\left( {N + k\log N}\right)$ time.

引理2.2. 给定无环连接$Q$、输入规模为$N$的数据库实例$\mathbf{I}$及元组$\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，在欧氏度量下，可以$O\left( N\right)$时间内计算出$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中距离$\theta$最近（或最远）的邻居。对于$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的每个元组，可在$O\left( {\log N}\right)$延迟内按与$\theta$距离的升序或降序枚举。此外，给定向量$u \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，可在$O\left( {N + k\log N}\right)$时间内计算出$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中与$u$内积最大的$k$个元组。

Rectangular oracle. Next, we focus on counting and enumerating the join results that lie in a rectangle. Let $\psi  \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ be an axis-parallel rectangle,defined as the product of $d$ intervals,i.e., $\psi  = {\psi }_{1} \times  \ldots  \times  {\psi }_{d}$ ,where ${\psi }_{j} = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{j},{b}_{j}}\right\rbrack$ and ${a}_{j},{b}_{j} \in  \mathbb{R}$ . A tuple $t$ lies in $\psi$ if and only if $t.{A}_{j} \in  {\psi }_{j} \Leftrightarrow$ ${a}_{j} \leq  t.{A}_{j} \leq  {b}_{j}$ ,for every ${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$ . Hence,a rectangle $\psi$ defines a predicate ${a}_{j} \leq  {A}_{j} \leq  {b}_{j}$ for each attribute ${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$ . Given a rectangle $\psi$ ,we can find all tuples in $\mathbf{I}$ that pass the predicate in $O\left( N\right)$ time and then apply Yannakakis algorithm [65] to count the number of result tuples in $\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,or apply the index from [15] to enumerate the result tuples in $\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

矩形预言器。接下来我们专注于统计和枚举位于矩形内的连接结果。设$\psi  \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$为轴平行矩形，定义为$d$个区间的乘积，即$\psi  = {\psi }_{1} \times  \ldots  \times  {\psi }_{d}$，其中${\psi }_{j} = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{j},{b}_{j}}\right\rbrack$且${a}_{j},{b}_{j} \in  \mathbb{R}$。元组$t$位于$\psi$当且仅当对每个${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$满足$t.{A}_{j} \in  {\psi }_{j} \Leftrightarrow$${a}_{j} \leq  t.{A}_{j} \leq  {b}_{j}$。因此，矩形$\psi$为每个属性${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$定义了谓词${a}_{j} \leq  {A}_{j} \leq  {b}_{j}$。给定矩形$\psi$，我们可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内找出$\mathbf{I}$中满足谓词的所有元组，随后应用Yannakakis算法[65]统计$\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的结果元组数量，或应用文献[15]的索引枚举$\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的结果元组。

LEMMA 2.3. For an acyclic join $Q$ ,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,and a rectangle $\psi  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,an index of size $O\left( N\right)$ can be constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time such that i) the number of result tuples in $\psi  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be returned in $O\left( N\right)$ time; and ii) all result tuples in $\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be enumerated with $O\left( 1\right)$ delay.

引理2.3. 对于无环连接$Q$、输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$及矩形$\psi  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构建大小为$O\left( N\right)$的索引，使得：i)可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内返回$\psi  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的结果元组数量；ii)能以$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举$\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的所有结果元组。

## 3 Cohesive Summaries

## 3 内聚性摘要

In this section,we present algorithms to construct cohesive $k$ -summaries for the Euclidean and Hamming metrics. Recall that any algorithm presented in this section is also an algorithm for the relational $k$ -center clustering problem.

本节将提出为欧氏度量和汉明度量构建内聚性$k$-摘要的算法。需注意，本节提出的任何算法同样适用于关系型$k$-中心聚类问题。

算法1：内聚欧氏$\left( {\mathbf{Q},\mathbf{I},k,\varepsilon }\right)$

$\mathbf{1}\left( {S,r,\beta }\right)  \leftarrow  \operatorname{ConstantApprox}\left( {Q,\mathbf{I},k}\right)$ ;

$2{P}_{\varepsilon } \leftarrow  \operatorname{CORESET}\left( {Q,\mathbf{I},S,r,\beta ,\varepsilon }\right) ;$

		$3{S}^{ * } \leftarrow$ FEDERGREENE $\left( {{P}_{\varepsilon },k}\right) \left\lbrack  {38}\right\rbrack$ ;

		$3{S}^{ * } \leftarrow$ 费德格林$\left( {{P}_{\varepsilon },k}\right) \left\lbrack  {38}\right\rbrack$；

4 return ${S}^{ * }$ ;

### 3.1 Euclidean Metric

### 3.1 欧氏度量

Our main algorithm for constructing a cohesive summary for Euclidean distance consists of three steps. In the first step,we compute a $\beta$ - cohesive $k$ -summary $S$ for some constant $\beta  \geq  1$ , along with a number $r$ such that ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \beta  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Next, $S$ is used to construct a small set ${P}_{\varepsilon } \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ (called $\varepsilon$ -coreset) such that the optimally cohesive $k$ -summary in ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ is an $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . In the last step,we run a 2-approximation algorithm (Feder and Greene algorithm [38]) for the cohesive summary (in the non-relational setting) on ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ ,and we derive the final result. Throughout the paper,we use the term non-relational setting to denote the case where all input data is given in one relation.

我们构建欧氏距离一致性摘要的主要算法包含三个步骤。第一步，针对某个常数$\beta  \geq  1$计算$\beta$-一致性$k$-摘要$S$，同时得到一个满足${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \beta  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$条件的数值$r$。接着利用$S$构建小型集合${P}_{\varepsilon } \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$（称为$\varepsilon$-核心集），使得${P}_{\varepsilon }$中的最优一致性$k$-摘要成为$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right)$-一致性$k$-摘要。最后一步，我们在${P}_{\varepsilon }$上运行非关系型场景下的2-近似算法（Feder和Greene算法[38]）生成一致性摘要，并导出最终结果。本文中，"非关系型场景"特指所有输入数据均存储于单一关系中的情况。

The pseudocode of our main algorithm is shown in Algorithm 1. In the next subsections, we show all three steps in detail. In Subsections 3.1.1 and 3.1.2, we show two different constant approximations algorithms for the cohesive summary. We can invoke any of them as the ConstantApprox(Q,I,k) procedure in Algorithm 1. Even though they both return a constant approximation, we present both because neither dominates the other in terms of running time. The first one runs in roughly $O\left( {{k}^{2}N}\right)$ time while the second one runs in $O\left( {{kN} + {k}^{O\left( d\right) }}\right)$ time. Next,in Subsection 3.1.3,we present the Coreset $\left( \cdot \right)$ procedure that gets as input the output of the previous constant approximation algorithm and constructs an $\varepsilon$ coreset ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ of size $O\left( {{\varepsilon }^{-d}k}\right)$ . Finally,in Subsection 3.1.4,we run a 2-approximation algorithm for the cohesive summary (in the non-relational setting) on ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ ,and show the final result. All missing proofs in this section are given in Appendix B.1.

主算法伪代码见算法1。后续小节将详细展示三个步骤：3.1.1与3.1.2节分别给出两种不同的常数近似算法实现，均可作为算法1中的ConstantApprox(Q,I,k)过程调用。虽然二者均返回常数近似解，但由于时间复杂度各具优势（前者耗时约$O\left( {{k}^{2}N}\right)$，后者耗时$O\left( {{kN} + {k}^{O\left( d\right) }}\right)$），故同时呈现。3.1.3节介绍Coreset$\left( \cdot \right)$过程，该过程将前步所得常数近似解作为输入，构建大小为$O\left( {{\varepsilon }^{-d}k}\right)$的$\varepsilon$核心集${P}_{\varepsilon }$。最终在3.1.4节，我们对${P}_{\varepsilon }$运行非关系型场景下的2-近似算法获得一致性摘要，并呈现最终结果。本节所有缺失证明详见附录B.1。

#### 3.1.1 Constant cohesive summary: Tree-based approximation.

#### 3.1.1 常数一致性摘要：基于树的近似方法

In this part, we describe a hierarchical approach for constructing a cohesive summary.

本部分描述构建一致性摘要的层次化方法

Main ideas. Consider an acyclic join $Q$ and a database I. Let ${\mathrm{A}}_{x},{\mathrm{\;A}}_{y} \subseteq  \mathrm{A}$ be two disjoint subsets of attributes. Let ${S}_{x} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{x}\right| }$ be a ${r}_{x}$ -cohesive $k$ -summary of ${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{x}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ ,and ${S}_{y} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{y}\right| }$ be a ${r}_{y}$ -cohesive $k$ -summary of ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{y}}Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . We show that a set $S \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}\right| }$ of size $k$ can be computed efficiently with

核心思路。设无环连接$Q$和数据库I，令${\mathrm{A}}_{x},{\mathrm{\;A}}_{y} \subseteq  \mathrm{A}$为两个不相交属性子集。若${S}_{x} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{x}\right| }$是${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{x}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$的${r}_{x}$-一致性$k$-摘要，${S}_{y} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{y}\right| }$是${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{y}}Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的${r}_{y}$-一致性$k$-摘要，则可高效计算出规模为$k$的集合$S \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}\right| }$，其满足：

small cohesion with respect to ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Let

相对于${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的小内聚度。设

$$
\bar{S} = \left\{  {t \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y} : \exists {t}^{\prime } \in  {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathbf{A}}_{y}}Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) ,\phi \left( {t,{t}^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}  }\right\}  ,
$$

be the set of tuples from the Cartesian product of two summaries that are "near" to some result tuple in ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . If $S$ is a 2-cohesive $k$ - summary of $\bar{S}$ ,a key property we show is that $S$ is a ${10}\sqrt{2}\max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ -cohesive $k$ -summary of ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . To construct a cohesive summary for ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,it suffices to construct a cohesive summary for ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ separately, further select a few representatives from the Cartesian product of these two summaries carefully,and finally return a $k$ - cohesive summary for those representatives.

为来自两个摘要笛卡尔积的元组集合，这些元组"接近"${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的某个结果元组。若$S$是$\bar{S}$的2-内聚$k$-摘要，我们证明的关键性质是：$S$也是${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的${10}\sqrt{2}\max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$-内聚$k$-摘要。要为${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x} \cup  {\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$构建内聚摘要，只需分别为${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{x}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$和${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$构建内聚摘要，再从这两个摘要的笛卡尔积中精心挑选若干代表元组，最终返回这些代表元组的$k$-内聚摘要。

Algorithm 2: Constant Approx_TREE(Q,I,k)

算法2：常数近似_TREE(Q,I,k)

---

	$\left( {{S}_{v},{r}_{v}}\right)  \leftarrow  \operatorname{ComputeRoot}\left( {Q,\mathrm{I},k}\right)$ ;

	$S \leftarrow  \varnothing$ ;

	foreach $\theta  \in  {S}_{v}$ do

	对每个$\theta  \in  {S}_{v}$执行

			foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ do

			对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$执行

				foreach $p \in  {R}_{i}$ do

				对每个$p \in  {R}_{i}$执行

						${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}}{\left( p \cdot  {A}_{j} - \theta  \cdot  {A}_{j}\right) }^{2};$

			$\overrightarrow{w} \leftarrow  \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle  ;$

			$Z \leftarrow$ index built for $Q,\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ as Lemma 2.1;

			根据引理2.1为$Q,\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$建立$Z \leftarrow$索引；

			${t}_{\theta } \leftarrow$ first result enumerated from $Z$ ;

			从$Z$枚举首个结果${t}_{\theta } \leftarrow$；

			$S \leftarrow  S \cup  \left\{  {t}_{\theta }\right\}  ;$

	while $\left| S\right|  < k$ do

	当$\left| S\right|  < k$时执行

			$t \leftarrow$ the next result enumerated from $Z$ ;

			从$Z$枚举下一个结果$t \leftarrow$；

			if $t \notin  S$ then $S \leftarrow  S \cup  \{ t\}$ ;

			若$t \notin  S$则$S \leftarrow  S \cup  \{ t\}$；

14 return $S$ with radius $r = 2 \cdot  {r}_{v}$ ;

14 返回半径$r = 2 \cdot  {r}_{v}$的$S$；

Our algorithm. Now, we are ready to describe our relational algorithm, with pseu-docode given in Algorithm 2 and a running example in Figure 1. Algorithm 2 first calls Algorithm 3 as a primitive to return a set ${S}_{v} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of size $k$ and a value ${r}_{v}$ such that the cohesion $\rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ is small and the value ${r}_{v}$ is a sufficiently small upper bound of $\rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . Notice that ${S}_{v}$ will not necessarily be a subset of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Then Algorithm 2 uses ${S}_{v}$ to construct a set $S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ that is a $O\left( 1\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary and a value $r$ such that ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ ,for a constant $\alpha$ .

我们的算法。现在，我们准备描述关系算法（伪代码见算法2，运行示例见图1）。算法2首先调用算法3作为基础过程，返回大小为$k$的集合${S}_{v} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$和值${r}_{v}$，使得内聚度$\rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$较小且${r}_{v}$是$\rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$足够小的上界。注意${S}_{v}$不一定是$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的子集。接着算法2利用${S}_{v}$构建$O\left( 1\right)$-内聚$k$-摘要集合$S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，并生成满足${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的值$r$（其中$\alpha$为常数）。

In Algorithm 3,we first construct a complete binary tree $\mathcal{T}$ with $m$ leaf nodes,where relation ${R}_{i}$ is stored at the $i$ -th leaf node. For each node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ ,we denote ${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{u} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{R}_{i}}$ is a descendant of $u{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}$ and ${\mathcal{Q}}_{u} = {\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{u}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . We visit all nodes in a bottom-up fashion,and for each node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ ,we compute i) a set ${S}_{u}$ of $k$ tuples in ${\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{u}\right| }$ with small cohesion $\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ ,and ii) a sufficiently small upper bound ${r}_{u}$ of $\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ ; see Lemma 3.4. Next,we show how to compute ${S}_{u}$ and ${r}_{u}$ for each node $u$ .

在算法3中，我们首先构建一棵具有$m$个叶节点的完全二叉树$\mathcal{T}$，其中关系${R}_{i}$存储在第$i$个叶节点。对于每个节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$，我们定义${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{u} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{R}_{i}}$是$u{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}$和${\mathcal{Q}}_{u} = {\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{u}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的后代节点。采用自底向上的方式遍历所有节点，并为每个节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$计算：i) ${\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{u}\right| }$中具有较小内聚度$\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$的$k$元组集合${S}_{u}$；ii) $\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的一个足够小的上界${r}_{u}$（参见引理3.4）。接着，我们将阐述如何为每个节点$u$计算${S}_{u}$和${r}_{u}$。

If $u$ is a leaf node that corresponds to relation ${R}_{i}$ ,we compute an approximate $k$ -summary ${S}_{u}$ for ${\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}\left( {R}_{i}\right)$ by invoking the algorithm in [38]. The cohesion of ${S}_{u}$ is denoted as ${r}_{u} = \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}\left( {R}_{i}\right) }\right)$ . If $u$ is an internal node,let $x,y$ be the two children of $u$ in $\mathcal{T}$ . Let ${S}_{x},{S}_{y}$ be the subsets of $k$ tuples we got from nodes $x,y$ ,respectively. Let ${r}^{ * } = \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ . We construct an approximate $k$ -summary ${S}_{u}$ using the tuples in ${S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$ . To check for each tuple $\theta  \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$ ,whether there exists any tuple in ${Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ within distance $\sqrt{2} \cdot  {r}^{ * }$ ,we use a nearest-neighbor oracle. For relation ${R}_{i}$ ,we define the weight function ${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$ as ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{A}}_{i} \cap  {\bar{A}}_{u}}}{\left( p{A}_{j} - \theta {A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$ ,where $p \in  {R}_{i}$ . We instantiate the index,defined by $Z$ ,for ranked enumeration from Lemma 2.1 with $\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},\ldots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$ as the vector of weight functions. Let ${t}_{\theta }$ be the first result tuple enumerated from $Z$ in ascending order $\left( {t}_{\theta }\right.$ is the nearest neighbor of $\theta$ in ${Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ). If $\phi \left( {\theta ,{t}_{\theta }}\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2}\max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ ,then we keep $\theta$ in ${\bar{S}}_{u}$ ; otherwise, we skip $\theta$ . Finally,we find a 2-cohesive $k$ -summary of ${\bar{S}}_{u}$ by invoking the Feder-Greene algorithm [38],denoted as ${S}_{u}$ ,and we set the upper bound ${r}_{u} = \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\bar{S}}_{u}}\right)  + \sqrt{2}\max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ . In the end,the Algorithm 3 returns the set ${S}_{v}$ and the upper bound ${r}_{v}$ ,for the root node $v$ of $\mathcal{T}$ ,to Algorithm 2 .

若$u$是对应关系${R}_{i}$的叶节点，则通过调用[38]中的算法计算${\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}\left( {R}_{i}\right)$的近似$k$-摘要${S}_{u}$。该摘要的凝聚度记为${r}_{u} = \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}\left( {R}_{i}\right) }\right)$。若$u$为内部节点，设$x,y$表示$\mathcal{T}$中$u$的两个子节点，${S}_{x},{S}_{y}$分别表示从节点$x,y$获取的$k$元组子集，令${r}^{ * } = \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$。我们利用${S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$中的元组构建近似$k$-摘要${S}_{u}$。为检查每个元组$\theta  \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$在${Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中是否存在距离$\sqrt{2} \cdot  {r}^{ * }$内的元组，采用最近邻查询器。对于关系${R}_{i}$，将权重函数${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$定义为${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{A}}_{i} \cap  {\bar{A}}_{u}}}{\left( p{A}_{j} - \theta {A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$，其中$p \in  {R}_{i}$。根据引理2.1，以$\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},\ldots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$作为权重函数向量实例化$Z$定义的索引进行排序枚举。设${t}_{\theta }$为按升序从$Z$枚举的首个结果元组（$\left( {t}_{\theta }\right.$是$\theta$在${Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的最近邻）。若$\phi \left( {\theta ,{t}_{\theta }}\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2}\max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$，则将$\theta$保留在${\bar{S}}_{u}$中；否则跳过$\theta$。最终通过调用Feder-Greene算法[38]找出${\bar{S}}_{u}$的2-凝聚$k$-摘要，记为${S}_{u}$，并设定上界${r}_{u} = \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\bar{S}}_{u}}\right)  + \sqrt{2}\max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$。算法3将根节点<b27></b7>的集合${S}_{v}$与上界${r}_{v}$返回给算法2。

Let $v$ be the root of $\mathcal{T}$ . Recall that ${S}_{v} \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ may not hold. To obtain a valid summary for $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ , in Algorithm 2,we visit every tuple $\theta  \in  {S}_{v}$ and find its nearest neighbor in $Q$ (I) using a similar nearest-neighbor oracle as we used before,constructing the index $Z$ . Let $S$ be the set of nearest neighbors we compute. If $\left| S\right|  < k$ ,we add arbitrary $k - \left| S\right|$ results from $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ to $S$ .

设$v$为$\mathcal{T}$的根节点。需注意${S}_{v} \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$可能不成立。为获得$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的有效摘要，在算法2中，我们遍历每个元组$\theta  \in  {S}_{v}$并通过类似之前使用的最近邻查询器，在$Q$(I)中寻找其最近邻，同时构建索引$Z$。令$S$表示计算的最近邻集合。若$\left| S\right|  < k$，则从$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中任意选取$k - \left| S\right|$个结果加入$S$。

Algorithm 3: ComputeRoot(Q,I,k)

算法3：计算根节点(Q,I,k)

---

Let $\mathcal{T}$ be a complete binary tree with $m$ leaf nodes;

设$\mathcal{T}$为具有$m$个叶节点的完全二叉树；

foreach leaf node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ do

对每个叶节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$执行：

			Suppose $u$ corresponds to relation ${R}_{i}$ ;

			假设$u$对应关系${R}_{i}$；

			${S}_{u} \leftarrow  k$ -summary of ${\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}{R}_{i}$ by algorithm

			通过[38]文献中的算法生成${\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}{R}_{i}$的${S}_{u} \leftarrow  k$摘要

				in [38];

							；

			${r}_{u} \leftarrow  \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\pi }_{{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}{R}_{i}}\right) ;$

	foreach internal node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ in bottom-up way do

	以自底向上方式遍历每个内部节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$：

			$x,y \leftarrow$ two children of $u$ in $\mathcal{T},{\bar{S}}_{u} \leftarrow  \varnothing$ ;

			$x,y \leftarrow$是$\mathcal{T},{\bar{S}}_{u} \leftarrow  \varnothing$中$u$的两个子节点；

			foreach $\theta  \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$ do

			对每个$\theta  \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$执行：

						foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ do

						对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$执行：

									foreach $p \in  {R}_{i}$ do

																	对每个$p \in  {R}_{i}$执行：

												${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i} \cap  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{u}}}{\left( p \cdot  {A}_{j} - \theta  \cdot  {A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$

						$\overrightarrow{w} \leftarrow  \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle  ;$

						$Z \leftarrow$ index built for ${Q}_{u},\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ as Lemma 2.1;

						根据引理2.1为${Q}_{u},\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$构建$Z \leftarrow$索引；

						${t}_{\theta } \leftarrow$ the first result enumerated from $Z$ ;

						${t}_{\theta } \leftarrow$是从$Z$枚举的首个结果；

						if $\phi \left( {{t}_{\theta },\theta }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ then

						若$\phi \left( {{t}_{\theta },\theta }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$则：

									${\bar{S}}_{u} \leftarrow  {\bar{S}}_{u} \cup  \{ \theta \}$

			${S}_{u} \leftarrow  k$ -summary of ${\bar{S}}_{u}$ by algorithm in [38];

			通过[38]文献中的算法生成${\bar{S}}_{u}$的${S}_{u} \leftarrow  k$摘要；

			${r}_{u} \leftarrow  \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\bar{S}}_{u}}\right)  + \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$

return $\left( {{S}_{v},{r}_{v}}\right)$ ;

返回 $\left( {{S}_{v},{r}_{v}}\right)$ ;

Fig. 1. A running example of Algorithm 3. Let $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right)$ with a database $\mathbf{I}$ as shown. Let $k = 2$ . The complete binary tree $\mathcal{T}$ is also shown with the root $v$ and two leaf nodes $x,y$ ,corresponding to ${R}_{1},{R}_{2}$ respectively. In line 4,Algorithm 3 first computes a 2-summary of ${R}_{1}$ as ${S}_{x}$ and a 2-summary of ${\pi }_{{A}_{3}}{R}_{2}$ as ${S}_{y}$ ,with ${r}_{x} = 2$ and ${r}_{y} = 1$ . It next investigates all tuples in ${S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$ ,and checks if there exists some tuple in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ within distance $2\sqrt{2}$ . It is easy to see ${\bar{S}}_{v} = {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$ . In line 17,it computes a 2-summary of ${\bar{S}}_{v}$ as ${S}_{v}$ . As $\left( {2,1,2}\right)  \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,it adds(2,1,2)to $S$ . As $\left( {1,2,2}\right)  \notin  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,it adds an arbitrary nearest neighbor(1,2,1)to $S$ . We observe that $\rho \left( {S,Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  = 2\sqrt{2}$ .

图1. 算法3的运行示例。设$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right)$的数据库为$\mathbf{I}$。设$k = 2$。完整二叉树$\mathcal{T}$如图所示，其根节点为$v$，两个叶节点分别为$x,y$，对应${R}_{1},{R}_{2}$。在第4行，算法3首先计算${R}_{1}$的2-摘要为${S}_{x}$，${\pi }_{{A}_{3}}{R}_{2}$的2-摘要为${S}_{y}$，其中${r}_{x} = 2$且${r}_{y} = 1$。接着检查${S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$中所有元组，验证是否存在$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的元组距离小于$2\sqrt{2}$。显然${\bar{S}}_{v} = {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$。第17行计算<b17></b7>的2-摘要为${S}_{v}$。由于$\left( {2,1,2}\right)  \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，将(2,1,2)加入$S$。因$\left( {1,2,2}\right)  \notin  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，任选最近邻(1,2,1)加入$S$。可见$\rho \left( {S,Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  = 2\sqrt{2}$。

Correctness. By Lemma 3.2,and Lemma 3.1,the nearest neighbor queries with any $\theta$ can be answered correctly.

正确性。根据引理3.2和引理3.1，对于任意$\theta$的最近邻查询均可正确应答。

LEMMA 3.1. For $\overrightarrow{w}$ at line 7 of Algorithm 2, $\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\theta }\right)$ for every $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

引理3.1. 对于算法2第7行的$\overrightarrow{w}$，每个$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$都满足$\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\theta }\right)$。

LEMMA 3.2. For $\overrightarrow{w}$ at line 12 of Algorithm 3, $\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\theta }\right)$ for every $t \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

引理3.2. 对于算法3第12行的$\overrightarrow{w}$，每个$t \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$都满足$\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\theta }\right)$。

Approximation. We need the following lemma to show the approximation ratio:

近似性. 需要以下引理证明近似比：

LEMMA 3.3. For any internal node $u$ with children $x,y$ in $\mathcal{T},\rho \left( {{\bar{S}}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ .

引理3.3. 对于$\mathcal{T},\rho \left( {{\bar{S}}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$中任意内部节点$u$及其子节点$x,y$。

Proof. For any node $x$ and its parent $u$ in $\mathcal{T}$ it holds that ${\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . For any tuple $p \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ there exists a tuple ${p}_{t} \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ such that ${p}_{t}.{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{x} = p$ . By definition,it also holds that ${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x} \subseteq  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{u}$ . Hence, ${\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . Consider an arbitrary tuple $t \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ . Let ${\theta }_{x} \in  {S}_{x}$ be the nearest tuple to $t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x}$ ,and ${\theta }_{y} \in  {S}_{y}$ be the nearest tuple to $t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{y}$ . By definition $\phi \left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x},{\theta }_{x}}\right)  \leq  {r}_{x}$ and $\phi \left( {t \cdot  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{y},{\theta }_{y}}\right)  \leq  {r}_{y}$ . We consider tuple $\theta$ as a concatenation of ${\theta }_{x}$ and ${\theta }_{y}$ . Then, $\phi \left( {t,\theta }\right)  =$ $\sqrt{{\phi }^{2}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x},{\theta }_{x}}\right)  + {\phi }^{2}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{y},{\theta }_{y}}\right) } \leq  \sqrt{{r}_{x}^{2} + {r}_{y}^{2}} \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ . In line 8 of Algorithm 3,we consider the tuple $\theta$ in one of the iterations. Since $\phi \left( {t,\theta }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ ,the condition in line 15 holds, because $\phi \left( {{t}_{\theta },\theta }\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,\theta }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ . Hence, $\theta$ is added in ${\bar{S}}_{u}$ . Overall,for each tuple $t \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ there exists a tuple in ${\bar{S}}_{u}$ within distance $\sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$ ,so the result follows. Next, we point out the invariants that are preserved in the execution of our algorithm:

证明。对于$\mathcal{T}$中的任意节点$x$及其父节点$u$，都满足${\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。对于任意元组$p \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，存在元组${p}_{t} \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$使得${p}_{t}.{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{x} = p$。根据定义，还满足${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x} \subseteq  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{u}$。因此，${\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。考虑任意元组$t \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，设${\theta }_{x} \in  {S}_{x}$是距离$t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x}$最近的元组，${\theta }_{y} \in  {S}_{y}$是距离$t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{y}$最近的元组。根据定义有$\phi \left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x},{\theta }_{x}}\right)  \leq  {r}_{x}$和$\phi \left( {t \cdot  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{y},{\theta }_{y}}\right)  \leq  {r}_{y}$。我们将元组$\theta$视为${\theta }_{x}$与${\theta }_{y}$的串联，则$\phi \left( {t,\theta }\right)  =$$\sqrt{{\phi }^{2}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{x},{\theta }_{x}}\right)  + {\phi }^{2}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{y},{\theta }_{y}}\right) } \leq  \sqrt{{r}_{x}^{2} + {r}_{y}^{2}} \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$。在算法3第8行中，我们在某次迭代中考察元组$\theta$。由于$\phi \left( {t,\theta }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$，第15行的条件成立，因为$\phi \left( {{t}_{\theta },\theta }\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,\theta }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$。因此，$\theta$被加入${\bar{S}}_{u}$。综上，对于每个元组$t \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，在${\bar{S}}_{u}$中都存在距离不超过$\sqrt{2} \cdot  \max \left\{  {{r}_{x},{r}_{y}}\right\}$的元组，故结论得证。接下来我们指出算法执行过程中保持的不变量：

LEMMA 3.4. For node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ at level $\ell ,\frac{1}{2} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  {\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\ell } \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ .

引理3.4. 对于层级$\ell ,\frac{1}{2} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  {\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\ell } \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$中的节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$。

Applying Lemma 3.4 to the root node $v$ ,we obtain $\rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{v} \leq  {\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\log m} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ and $\frac{1}{2} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \cdot  {}^{6}$ From lines 3-10 in Algorithm 2,we have $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {2\rho }\left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . Hence,we return a set $S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and a value $r$ such that $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  2{\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\log m} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ .

将引理3.4应用于根节点$v$，我们得到$\rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{v} \leq  {\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\log m} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$和$\frac{1}{2} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \cdot  {}^{6}$。根据算法2中第3-10行，可得$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {2\rho }\left( {{S}_{v},\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。因此，我们返回集合$S \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$和值$r$，使得$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  2{\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\log m} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。

Complexity. In Algorithm 3,it takes $O\left( {N\log k}\right)$ time to invoke the algorithm in [38] at line 4 . At line $8,\left| {{S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}}\right|  = O\left( {k}^{2}\right)$ . For each $\theta  \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$ ,it takes $O\left( N\right)$ time to construct $Z$ at line 13 and get the first result tuple ${t}_{\theta }$ within $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ time. It takes $O\left( {{k}^{2}\log k}\right)$ time to invoke the algorithm in [38] at line 17. The for-loop at lines $6 - {18}$ repeats $O\left( m\right)$ times,since there are $O\left( m\right)$ nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ . In Algorithm 2,for each tuple $\theta  \in  {S}_{v}$ for the root node $v$ ,it takes $O\left( N\right)$ time to construct $Z$ at line 8 and get the first result tuple ${t}_{\theta }$ within $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ time. Overall,our algorithm runs in $O\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{2}}\right)$ space.

复杂度分析. 在算法3中，第4行调用[38]的算法耗时$O\left( {N\log k}\right)$。第$8,\left| {{S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}}\right|  = O\left( {k}^{2}\right)$行。对于每个$\theta  \in  {S}_{x} \times  {S}_{y}$，第13行构建$Z$并获取首个结果元组${t}_{\theta }$耗时$O\left( N\right)$，在$O\left( {\log N}\right)$时间内完成。第17行调用[38]的算法耗时$O\left( {{k}^{2}\log k}\right)$。第$6 - {18}$行的for循环重复$O\left( m\right)$次，因为$\mathcal{T}$中存在$O\left( m\right)$个节点。在算法2中，对于根节点$v$的每个元组$\theta  \in  {S}_{v}$，第8行构建$Z$并获取首个结果元组${t}_{\theta }$耗时$O\left( N\right)$，在$O\left( {\log N}\right)$时间内完成。总体而言，我们的算法运行时间为$O\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$，空间复杂度为$O\left( {N + {k}^{2}}\right)$。

THEOREM 3.5. For an acyclic join $Q$ of $m$ relations,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ and $\alpha  = 2 \cdot  {\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\log m}$ , an $\alpha$ -cohesive $k$ -summary of $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ under Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{2}}\right)$ space,with $r$ such that ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ .

定理3.5：对于$Q$个$m$关系的无环连接，在输入规模为$\mathbf{I}$和$N$的数据库$\alpha  = 2 \cdot  {\left( {10}\sqrt{2}\right) }^{\log m}$中，欧几里得度量下$\alpha$的$k$凝聚摘要可在$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$时间内利用$O\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$空间计算得出，其中$O\left( {N + {k}^{2}}\right)$满足$r$。

#### 3.1.2 Constant cohesive summary: Geometry-based approximation.

#### 3.1.2 恒定凝聚摘要：基于几何的近似方法

Next, we exploit the properties of the Euclidean metric to obtain a different approximation algorithm.

接下来，我们利用欧几里得度量的特性来获得另一种近似算法。

Main ideas. Using the intuition from [43],

核心思路。根据文献[43]的启示，

Algorithm 4: Constant Approx_Geometry(Q,I,k)

算法4：恒定近似_几何方法(Q,I,k)

foreach ${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$ do

对每个${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$执行

	${a}_{j} \leftarrow  \mathop{\min }\limits_{{R}_{i}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{x \in  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}{R}_{i}}}x$ and ${b}_{j} \leftarrow  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{R}_{i}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \in  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}{R}_{i}}}x;$

	${a}_{j} \leftarrow  \mathop{\min }\limits_{{R}_{i}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{x \in  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}{R}_{i}}}x$与${b}_{j} \leftarrow  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{R}_{i}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \in  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}{R}_{i}}}x;$

$\Gamma  \leftarrow  { \times  }_{j : {A}_{j} \in  \mathrm{A}}\left\lbrack  {{a}_{j},{b}_{j}}\right\rbrack  ,h \leftarrow  0,{S}^{\prime } \leftarrow  \varnothing ,\mathcal{B} \leftarrow  \varnothing ;$

while $h \leq  {c}^{d} \cdot  k$ do

当$h \leq  {c}^{d} \cdot  k$时执行

	if $h = 0$ then ${q}_{h} \leftarrow$ an arbitrary tuple in $\Gamma$ ;

	若$h = 0$则${q}_{h} \leftarrow$取$\Gamma$中任意元组；

	else ${q}_{h} \leftarrow$ the point in $\Gamma$ farthest from ${S}^{\prime }$ ;

	否则${q}_{h} \leftarrow$取$\Gamma$中距离${S}^{\prime }$最远的点；

	foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ do

	对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$执行

			foreach $p \in  {R}_{i}$ do

			对每个$p \in  {R}_{i}$执行

				${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}}{\left( p \cdot  {A}_{j} - {q}_{h} \cdot  {A}_{j}\right) }^{2};$

	$\overrightarrow{w} \leftarrow  \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle  ;$

	$Z \leftarrow$ an index built for $Q,\mathrm{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ as Lemma 2.1;

	$Z \leftarrow$为$Q,\mathrm{I},\overrightarrow{w}$建立如引理2.1所述的索引；

	${s}_{h} \leftarrow$ the first result enumerated from $Z$ ;

	${s}_{h} \leftarrow$从$Z$枚举的首个结果；

	${\mathcal{B}}_{h} \leftarrow$ a ball centered at ${q}_{i}$ of radius $\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$ ;

	${\mathcal{B}}_{h} \leftarrow$以${q}_{i}$为中心、$\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$为半径的球体；

	${S}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {S}^{\prime } \cup  \left\{  {s}_{h}\right\}  ,\Gamma  \leftarrow  \Gamma  - {\mathcal{B}}_{h},\mathcal{B} \leftarrow  \mathcal{B} \cup  \left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{h}\right\}  ;$

	$h \leftarrow  h + 1$

$S \leftarrow$ a $k$ -summary of ${S}^{\prime }$ by algorithm in [38];

	$S \leftarrow$通过文献[38]算法生成的$k$摘要；

return $S$ ;

we design an algorithm in the relational setting. We compute a summary ${S}^{\prime }$ iteratively. Initially,let $\Gamma$ be a rectangle containing all tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and let ${S}^{\prime } = \varnothing$ . In each iteration, we solve a geometric problem computing the point ${q}_{h} \in  \Gamma$ farthest from the set ${S}^{\prime }$ (in the first iteration, ${q}_{1}$ is an arbitrary tuple in $\Gamma$ ). We next compute the tuple ${s}_{h} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ closest to ${q}_{h}$ and add it in ${S}^{\prime }$ . Then,we implicitly remove from $\Gamma$ a ball around ${q}_{i}$ with radius $\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$ so that no other tuple could be selected close to ${q}_{i}$ . We note that $\Gamma$ is not a rectangle after the first iteration. Instead, $\Gamma$ is the intersection of a rectangle with the complement of a set of balls. We use the nearest-neighbor oracle to compute the tuple in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ that is closest to ${q}_{h}$ .

我们在关系型数据库中设计了一种算法。通过迭代计算摘要${S}^{\prime }$。初始时，设$\Gamma$为包含$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有元组的矩形区域，并令${S}^{\prime } = \varnothing$。每次迭代中，我们求解一个几何问题：计算距集合${S}^{\prime }$最远的点${q}_{h} \in  \Gamma$（首次迭代时，${q}_{1}$是$\Gamma$中的任意元组）。接着找出距${q}_{h}$最近的元组${s}_{h} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，将其加入${S}^{\prime }$。然后，我们隐式地从$\Gamma$中移除以${q}_{i}$为中心、$\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$为半径的球体区域，确保不会选中${q}_{i}$邻近的其他元组。值得注意的是，首次迭代后$\Gamma$不再是矩形，而是矩形与若干球体补集的交集。我们使用最近邻查询器计算$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中距${q}_{h}$最近的元组。

Our algorithm. We next describe our algorithm in more detail with the pseu-docode given in Algorithm 4 and a running example in Figure 2. We start with incrementally add points to a set ${S}^{\prime }$ . Initially ${S}^{\prime } = \varnothing$ . Let $\mathcal{B} = \varnothing$ be a set of balls in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ (initially empty). We repeat the procedure ${c}^{d} \cdot  k$ times for a constant $c$ . In the first iteration,we choose an arbitrary point ${q}_{1}$ in $\Gamma$ . In $h$ -th iteration,for $h > 1$ ,we compute the point ${q}_{h} \in  \Gamma$ with the farthest

我们的算法。下面通过算法4的伪代码和图2的实例详细说明：逐步向集合${S}^{\prime }$添加点，初始状态${S}^{\prime } = \varnothing$。设$\mathcal{B} = \varnothing$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中的球体集合（初始为空）。对常数$c$重复该过程${c}^{d} \cdot  k$次。首次迭代时，从$\Gamma$任选一点${q}_{1}$；第$h$次迭代时（$h > 1$），计算距集合最远的点${q}_{h} \in  \Gamma$

${}^{6}$ We use $\log \left( \cdot \right)$ for the logarithmic function with base 2 .

${}^{6}$ 我们使用$\log \left( \cdot \right)$表示以2为底的对数函数。

---

Computing A Well-Representative Summary of Conjunctive Query Results distance from set ${S}^{\prime }$ . Next,by assigning weights $\overrightarrow{w}$ (similarly to Algorithm 2) we construct the ranked enumeration index $Z$ implementing a nearest-neighbor oracle. We find the nearest tuple in $\mathcal{Q}$ (I) from point ${q}_{h}$ ,denoted as ${s}_{h}$ ,and add ${s}_{h}$ into ${S}^{\prime }$ . Let ${\mathcal{B}}_{h}$ be the ball of radius $\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$ centered at ${q}_{h}$ . Set $\mathcal{B} = \mathcal{B} \cup  \left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{h}\right\}$ and $\Gamma  = \Gamma  - {\mathcal{B}}_{h}$ . We implicitly remove all result tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ "covered" by ${q}_{h}$ within radius $\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$ . After the procedure above,we get a set ${S}^{\prime }$ of ${c}^{d} \cdot  k$ candidate centers. At last,we compute a cohesive $k$ -center $S$ of ${S}^{\prime }$ by invoking the algorithm in [38].

计算连接查询结果的代表性摘要 首先计算距集合${S}^{\prime }$的最远距离。接着通过分配权重$\overrightarrow{w}$（类似算法2），构建实现最近邻查询器的排序枚举索引$Z$。从点${q}_{h}$找出$\mathcal{Q}$(I)中的最近元组${s}_{h}$，将其加入${S}^{\prime }$。设${\mathcal{B}}_{h}$是以${q}_{h}$为中心、$\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$为半径的球体。更新$\mathcal{B} = \mathcal{B} \cup  \left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{h}\right\}$和$\Gamma  = \Gamma  - {\mathcal{B}}_{h}$。我们隐式移除$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中被${q}_{h}$在半径$\phi \left( {{q}_{h},{s}_{h}}\right)$范围内"覆盖"的所有结果元组。最终获得含${c}^{d} \cdot  k$个候选中心的集合${S}^{\prime }$，最后调用文献[38]算法计算${S}^{\prime }$的凝聚$k$中心$S$。
