Correctness. By Lemma 3.6,the nearest neighbor of every point ${q}_{h} \in  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be correctly found.

正确性。根据引理3.6，每个点${q}_{h} \in  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的最近邻都能被准确找到。

LEMMA 3.6. For $\overrightarrow{w}$ at line 10 of Algorithm 4, $\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,{q}_{h}}\right)$ for every tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

引理3.6。对于算法4第10行中的$\overrightarrow{w}$，每个元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$都满足$\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,{q}_{h}}\right)$。

The correctness of our algorithm follows from Lemma 3.6 and [43].

本算法的正确性由引理3.6和文献[43]保证。

Approximation. From [43],we know $\rho \left( {{S}^{\prime },Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  4{\rho }_{k}\left( {Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . From [38],we know that $S$ is a 2-cohesive $k$ -summary of ${S}^{\prime }$ ,i.e., $\rho \left( {S,{S}^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {S}^{\prime }\right)$ . Moreover, ${\rho }_{k}\left( {S}^{\prime }\right)  \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ since ${S}^{\prime } \subseteq$ $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Together, $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,{S}^{\prime }}\right)  + \rho \left( {{S}^{\prime },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {S}^{\prime }\right)  + 4{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  6{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Hence, Algorithm 4 returns a 6-cohesive $k$ -summary.

近似性。由文献[43]可知$\rho \left( {{S}^{\prime },Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  4{\rho }_{k}\left( {Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。根据文献[38]，$S$是${S}^{\prime }$的2-内聚$k$摘要，即$\rho \left( {S,{S}^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {S}^{\prime }\right)$。此外，由于${S}^{\prime } \subseteq$$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，故${\rho }_{k}\left( {S}^{\prime }\right)  \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。综上可得$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,{S}^{\prime }}\right)  + \rho \left( {{S}^{\prime },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {S}^{\prime }\right)  + 4{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  6{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。因此，算法4返回的是6-内聚$k$摘要。

Complexity. The initialization phase takes $O\left( N\right)$ time. In $h$ -th iteration of the while-loop,it takes $O\left( N\right)$ time to construct $Z$ and get the first result ${s}_{h}$ within $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ time. Furthermore,there are $O\left( k\right)$ balls ${\mathcal{B}}_{h}$ ’s and $O\left( k\right)$ points in $S$ . The point ${q}_{h}$ (farthest point from the current ${S}^{\prime }$ in $\Gamma$ ) can be computed in $O\left( {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}\right)$ time [8]. At last,invoking the algorithm in [38] takes $O\left( {k\log k}\right)$ time, since $\left| {S}^{\prime }\right|  = O\left( k\right)$ . It computes the union of $k$ balls in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,so the total space is $O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil }}\right)$ .

复杂度。初始化阶段耗时$O\left( N\right)$。在while循环的第$h$次迭代中，构建$Z$并在$O\left( {\log N}\right)$时间内获取首个结果${s}_{h}$需时$O\left( N\right)$。此外，存在$O\left( k\right)$个球体${\mathcal{B}}_{h}$和$S$中的$O\left( k\right)$个点。点${q}_{h}$（即$\Gamma$中距当前${S}^{\prime }$最远的点）可在$O\left( {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}\right)$时间内计算得出[8]。最后调用文献[38]算法耗时$O\left( {k\log k}\right)$，因为$\left| {S}^{\prime }\right|  = O\left( k\right)$。该算法计算${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中$k$个球体的并集，故总空间复杂度为$O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil }}\right)$。

THEOREM 3.7. For an acyclic join $Q$ with $d$ attributes and a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,a 6-cohesive $k$ -summary in Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$ space.

定理3.7。对于具有$d$个属性的无环连接$Q$及输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，可在$O\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$时间内利用$O\left( {N + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right)$空间计算出欧氏度量下的6-内聚$k$摘要。

#### 3.1.3 Coreset.

#### 3.1.3 核心集

Coresets for a set of points have been well studied [9], but these algorithms require direct access to $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,which we do not have. Chen et al. [25] constructed coresets for risk minimization problems over relational data, but its complexity or utility depends on the diameter of the query results in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Below,we show how to improve this result.

针对点集的核集(coreset)已有深入研究[9]，但这些算法需要直接访问$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，而我们不具备此条件。Chen等人[25]构建了关系数据风险最小化问题的核集，但其复杂度或效用取决于$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中查询结果的直径。下文我们将展示如何改进这一结果。

Definition 3.8 (Coreset). For an acyclic join $Q$ and a database I,an integer $k \in  {\mathbb{N}}^{ + }$ ,and a parameter $\varepsilon  > 0$ ,a subset ${P}_{\varepsilon } \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ is an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for the cohesive summary if $\rho \left( {{S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right)  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ , where ${S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * } \subseteq  {P}_{\varepsilon }$ is the optimal cohesive $k$ -summary for ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ ,i.e., $\rho \left( {{S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * },{P}_{\varepsilon }}\right)  = {\rho }_{k}\left( {P}_{\varepsilon }\right)$ .

定义3.8（核集）。对于无环连接$Q$、数据库I、整数$k \in  {\mathbb{N}}^{ + }$和参数$\varepsilon  > 0$，当满足$\rho \left( {{S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right)  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$时，子集${P}_{\varepsilon } \subseteq  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$称为内聚摘要的$\varepsilon$-核集，其中${S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * } \subseteq  {P}_{\varepsilon }$是${P}_{\varepsilon }$的最优内聚$k$-摘要，即$\rho \left( {{S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * },{P}_{\varepsilon }}\right)  = {\rho }_{k}\left( {P}_{\varepsilon }\right)$。

Our algorithm. Let $S$ be an $O\left( 1\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and $r$ be a small enough quantity such that $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r$ . We compute a grid over ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with a sufficiently small diagonal (roughly $\varepsilon  \cdot  r$ ) and find the set of non-empty cells in the grid. For each such cell $\square$ ,we run the rectangular oracle (Lemma 2.3) to get a tuple in $\square  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{\;I}\right)$ . Finally,we return the set of tuples selected by the rectangular oracle in the non-empty cells.

我们的算法。令$S$作为$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$O\left( 1\right)$-内聚$k$-摘要，$r$为足够小的量使得$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r$。我们在${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$上计算具有足够小对角线（约$\varepsilon  \cdot  r$）的网格，找出网格中非空单元格集合。对每个非空单元格$\square$，运行矩形预言机（引理2.3）获取$\square  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{\;I}\right)$中的元组。最终返回由矩形预言机在非空单元格中选择的元组集合。

We next describe our algorithm in more detail

接下来我们将更详细地描述算法

---

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 5: Coreset $\left( {Q,\mathbf{I},S,r,\beta ,\varepsilon }\right)$

算法5：核集$\left( {Q,\mathbf{I},S,r,\beta ,\varepsilon }\right)$

---

${P}_{\varepsilon } \leftarrow  \varnothing$ ;

$G \leftarrow$ a grid in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with cell diagonal $\frac{\varepsilon  \cdot  r}{\beta }$ ;

在${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中建立单元格对角线为$\frac{\varepsilon  \cdot  r}{\beta }$的网格

foreach $y \in  S$ do

遍历$y \in  S$时执行

	${\mathcal{B}}_{y} \leftarrow$ ball of radius $r$ centered at $y$ ;

	以$y$为中心、$r$为半径的球体

	${G}_{y} \leftarrow  \left\{  {\psi  \in  G : \psi  \cap  {\mathcal{B}}_{y} \neq  \varnothing }\right\}  ;$

foreach $\psi  \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{G}_{y}$ do

遍历$\psi  \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{G}_{y}$时执行

	if $\psi  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ then

	若满足$\psi  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$则

		${p}_{\psi } \leftarrow$ arbitrary tuple in $\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ;

		选取$\psi  \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中任意元组

		${P}_{\varepsilon } \leftarrow  {P}_{\varepsilon } \cup  \left\{  {p}_{\psi }\right\}$

return ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ ;

返回${P}_{\varepsilon }$

with the pseudocode given in Algorithm 5 and an example in Figure 3. Using Theorem 3.5 (or Theorem 3.7),we take as input a $\beta$ -cohesive $k$ - summary $S$ for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ for constant $\beta  > 1$ ,and a value $r$ such that ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq$ $\beta  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . We first construct a grid $G$ in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with cell diagonal length $\frac{\varepsilon  \cdot  r}{\beta }$ and find out all non-empty cells in $G$ ,i.e.,those contain at least one tuple in $\mathbf{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ . Instead of visiting every tuple in $\mathbf{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ to locate non-empty cells, which is too expensive, we resort to the cohesive summary $S$ . Recall that for every tuple $x \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ ,there exists some $y \in  S$ such that $\phi \left( {x,y}\right)  \leq  r$ . For every $y \in  S$ ,let ${\mathcal{B}}_{y}$ be the ball centered at $y$ of radius $r$ . Let ${G}_{y} \subseteq  G$ be the set of cells covered or partially intersected by ${\mathcal{B}}_{y}$ . At last,we visit every cell in $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{G}_{y}$ and include an arbitrary tuple in the non-empty cells using the rectangular oracle as its representative. The set of all representatives is the coreset.

根据算法5提供的伪代码及图3示例，运用定理3.5（或定理3.7），我们输入一个$\beta$-内聚的$k$-摘要$S$（针对常数$\beta  > 1$）以及满足${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq$$\beta  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的数值$r$。首先在${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中构建单元格对角线长度为$\frac{\varepsilon  \cdot  r}{\beta }$的网格$G$，并标记所有非空单元格（即包含$\mathbf{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$中至少一个元组的单元格）。为避免遍历$\mathbf{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$中每个元组定位非空单元格的高开销，我们利用内聚摘要$S$。注意到对于每个元组$x \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，存在$y \in  S$使得$\phi \left( {x,y}\right)  \leq  r$。对于每个$y \in  S$，令${\mathcal{B}}_{y}$表示以$y$为中心、$r$为半径的球体，${G}_{y} \subseteq  G$为被${\mathcal{B}}_{y}$完全覆盖或部分相交的单元格集合。最终遍历$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{G}_{y}$中每个单元格，使用矩形预言机选取非空单元格内任意元组作为代表，所有代表元组构成核心集。

Correctness. Since $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\mathcal{B}}_{y}$ . Hence,every tuple in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ lies in one cell in $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{G}_{y}$ . As above,let ${S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * } \subseteq  {P}_{\varepsilon }$ be the optimally cohesive $k$ -summary of ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ . We have

正确性证明。由于$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\mathcal{B}}_{y}$，故$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中每个元组均位于$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{G}_{y}$的某个单元格内。设${S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * } \subseteq  {P}_{\varepsilon }$为${P}_{\varepsilon }$的最优内聚$k$-摘要，可得：

$$
\rho \left( {{S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {{S}_{\varepsilon }^{ * },{P}_{\varepsilon }}\right)  + \frac{\varepsilon r}{\beta } \leq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + \frac{\varepsilon r}{\beta } \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right)  \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) ,
$$

where the last inequality follows from the fact that $r \leq  \beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Hence, ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ is an $\varepsilon$ -coreset of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

末项不等式成立依据$r \leq  \beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$，因此${P}_{\varepsilon }$构成$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$\varepsilon$-核心集。

Complexity. As any ball of radius $r$ covers and partially intersects $O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-d}\right)$ grid cells with diagonal length ${\varepsilon r}/\beta$ ,we have $\left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right|  = O\left( {k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ . We execute rectangular oracles for $O\left( {k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ cells. Each query takes $O\left( N\right)$ time. Hence,Algorithm 5 runs in $O\left( {{kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time.

复杂度分析。由于半径为$r$的球体最多覆盖或部分相交$O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-d}\right)$个对角线长度为${\varepsilon r}/\beta$的网格单元格，故$\left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right|  = O\left( {k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$。我们对$O\left( {k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$个单元格执行矩形预言机查询，每次耗时$O\left( N\right)$，因此算法5的时间复杂度为$O\left( {{kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$。

THEOREM 3.9. For an acyclic join $Q$ with $d$ attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,and a parameter $\varepsilon  > 0$ ,an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for cohesive summaries under Euclidean distance of $O\left( {{\varepsilon }^{-d}k}\right)$ size can be constructed in $O\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d},N{\log }^{2}N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}  }\right)$ time.

定理3.9. 对于具有$d$属性的无环连接$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，以及参数$\varepsilon  > 0$，可以在$O\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d},N{\log }^{2}N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}  }\right)$时间内构建出欧氏距离下$O\left( {{\varepsilon }^{-d}k}\right)$规模的凝聚摘要$\varepsilon$-核心集。

Remark 1. The min term in the time complexity in Theorem 3.9 depends on the algorithm used for computing cohesive summaries (Theorem 3.5 or Theorem 3.7).

注记1. 定理3.9时间复杂度中的最小值项取决于计算凝聚摘要所用的算法（定理3.5或定理3.7）。

Remark 2. We note that if the value $r$ is unknown (as in the algorithm of Theorem 3.7),it suffices to run a binary search on the ${L}_{\infty }$ distances of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . For each candidate ${r}^{\prime }$ ,we check whether the set of balls $\left\{  {\mathcal{B}\left( {y,\sqrt{d}{r}^{\prime }}\right)  \mid  y \in  S}\right\}$ cover all tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ by running rectangular oracles on the grids intersected by the balls. We repeat this procedure until we find the smallest value ${r}^{\prime }$ that satisfies the condition. We describe the details in Appendix B.1.

注记2. 我们注意到，若$r$值未知（如定理3.7算法所示），只需对$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的${L}_{\infty }$距离进行二分搜索。对每个候选值${r}^{\prime }$，我们通过在被球体相交的网格上运行矩形预言机，检查球集$\left\{  {\mathcal{B}\left( {y,\sqrt{d}{r}^{\prime }}\right)  \mid  y \in  S}\right\}$是否覆盖$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有元组。重复此过程直至找到满足条件的最小${r}^{\prime }$值。详见附录B.1。

#### 3.1.4 Implications to cohesive summaries and relational $k$ -center clustering.

#### 3.1.4 对凝聚摘要与关系型$k$-中心聚类的影响

Let ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ be the $\varepsilon$ -coreset obtained. We simply invoke the Feder-Greene algorithm [38] on ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ . Let ${S}^{ * }$ be the set of $k$ centers returned. By definition $\rho \left( {{S}^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ . We obtain:

设${P}_{\varepsilon }$为获得的$\varepsilon$-核心集。我们直接在${P}_{\varepsilon }$上调用Feder-Greene算法[38]。令${S}^{ * }$为返回的$k$个中心点集合。根据定义$\rho \left( {{S}^{ * },\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。可得：

THEOREM 3.10. For an acyclic join $Q$ with $d$ attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,and a parameter $\varepsilon  > 0$ ,a $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ under Euclidean distance can be computed in $O\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d},N{\log }^{2}N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}  }\right)$ time. The same guarantees hold for the relational $k$ -center clustering problem.

定理3.10. 对于具有$d$属性的无环连接$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，以及参数$\varepsilon  > 0$，可在$O\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d},N{\log }^{2}N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}  }\right)$时间内计算出欧氏距离下$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$-凝聚$k$-摘要。该保证同样适用于关系型$k$-中心聚类问题。

Remark. The space needed by our algorithms in Theorems 3.9 and 3.10 depends on the $O\left( 1\right)$ - cohesive $k$ -summary algorithm used for coreset construction. It is $O\left( {N + {k}^{2} + k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ (resp. $O(N +$ ${k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1} + k{\varepsilon }^{-d})$ if the algorithm in Section 3.1.1 (resp. Section 3.1.2) is used.

注记. 定理3.9和3.10中算法所需空间取决于核心集构建所用的$O\left( 1\right)$-凝聚$k$-摘要算法。若采用3.1.1节（或3.1.2节）算法，则为$O\left( {N + {k}^{2} + k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$（相应地，$O(N +$${k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1} + k{\varepsilon }^{-d})$）。

tain a $O\left( 1\right)$ -cohesive summary under the Hamming metric. The maximum Hamming distance between two tuples from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ is $d = O\left( 1\right)$ . Hence,an algorithm that chooses $k$ arbitrary tuples from $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ returns a $d$ -cohesive $k$ -summary. However, this approximation ratio is rather unsatisfactory. The algorithm in Section 3.1 does not work for the Hamming metric mainly because the coreset construction only applies to the Euclidean metric. Hence, we need separate techniques for constructing a 2-cohesive $k$ - summary under the Hamming metric.

在汉明度量下获得$O\left( 1\right)$-内聚摘要。$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中两个元组间的最大汉明距离为$d = O\left( 1\right)$。因此，从$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$中随机选取$k$个元组的算法可生成$d$-内聚的$k$-摘要。但该近似比率并不理想。3.1节算法不适用于汉明度量，因为核心集构建仅适用于欧氏度量。因此我们需要专门技术来构建汉明度量下的2-内聚$k$-摘要。

Main ideas. Suppose the value of ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ is known in advance. We repeat the following step for $k$ iterations: we choose an arbitrary tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ , add it to the $k$ -summary $S$ ,and remove all items within distance $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . The resulting $S$ is a 2-cohesive summary for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . However,it is expensive to explicitly remove tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,which requires materializing $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Instead,we compute a set of non-intersecting rectangles such that any point selected from these rectangles has a distance greater than $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ (or equivalently at least $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$ ) from the previously selected tuples $S$ . For every new tuple $t$ we insert in $S$ ,we choose a set ${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ of $O\left( {2}^{d}\right)  = O\left( 1\right)$ non-intersecting (open) rectangles around $t$ such that the union of these rectangles defines the points with distance at least $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$ from $t$ . Let $\mathcal{R} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ . In order to decide the next tuple to add in $S$ ,we only visit the regions in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with distance at least $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$ from all tuples in $S$ . One crucial observation is that these regions are covered by exactly $\left| S\right|$ rectangles in $\mathcal{R}$ . We rely on the rectangular oracle in Section 2 to find tuples from $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ that fall into these regions.

核心思路。假设${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的值已知。我们对$k$次迭代重复以下步骤：任选一个元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$加入$k$-摘要$S$，并移除$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中距离$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$内的所有项。最终$S$即为$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的2-凝聚摘要。但显式删除$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的元组代价高昂，需物化$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$。取而代之，我们计算一组不相交矩形，使得从这些矩形中选择的任何点与已选元组$S$的距离大于$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$（或等价于至少$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$）。对于每个新插入$S$的元组$t$，我们在其周围选择$O\left( {2}^{d}\right)  = O\left( 1\right)$个不相交（开）矩形集合${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$，这些矩形的并集定义了与$t$距离至少为$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$的点集。设$\mathcal{R} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$。为确定下一个待添加元组，我们仅访问${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中与$S$所有元组距离至少$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$的区域。关键发现是这些区域恰好被$\mathcal{R}$中的$\left| S\right|$个矩形覆盖。我们借助第2节的矩形预言器从$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中定位落入这些区域的元组。

Our algorithm. We next describe our algorithm in more detail with the pseudocode given in Algorithm 6. For an attribute ${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$ ,let $\mathbf{I}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  = \left\{  {{\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( {R}_{i}\right)  \mid  {R}_{i} \in  \mathbf{R}}\right\}$ and let $\gamma$ be half of the minimum non-zero difference between two values in $\mathbf{I}\left( {A}_{j}\right)$ . Let $\mathcal{R} = \varnothing$ be a set of rectangles that initially is empty. To identify the next tuple to insert in $S$ ,we construct a rectangular decomposition of the union of rectangles in $\mathcal{R}$ . The rectangular decomposition [10] of $\mathcal{R}$ ,denoted $\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ ,is a partitioning of the union of the rectangles in $\mathcal{R}$ into rectangular contiguous regions,called cells, such that for each cell $\tau$ ,every point in $\tau$ lies in the same subset of $\mathcal{R}$ .

算法实现。下面通过算法6的伪代码详细描述。对于属性${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$，令$\mathbf{I}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  = \left\{  {{\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( {R}_{i}\right)  \mid  {R}_{i} \in  \mathbf{R}}\right\}$为$\mathbf{I}\left( {A}_{j}\right)$中两非零值最小差的一半，$\gamma$初始为空矩形集。为确定下一个待插入$S$的元组，我们对$\mathcal{R}$中矩形的并集进行矩形分解。该分解[10]记为$\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$，将$\mathcal{R}$的并集划分为连续矩形区域（称为单元），每个单元$\tau$内的所有点都位于$\mathcal{R}$的相同子集中。

Since,we do not know the optimum cohesion and ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \in  \{ 1,\ldots ,d\}$ ,we try every value ${r}_{H} \in  \{ 1,2,\ldots ,d\}$ as a guess for value $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$ . We repeat the following step for (at most) $k$ iterations. In the $i$ -th iteration,we visit every cell in $\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ until we find one with density $i$ (a cell that is contained in exactly $i$ rectangles). Let $C$ be a cell with density $i$ such that $\left| {C \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ . We get an arbitrary tuple $t \in  C \cap  Q$ (I) using the rectangular oracle. If $i = 1,C$ is a rectangle containing all tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and $t$ is any arbitrary tuple in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Next,we construct a set ${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ of $O\left( 1\right)$ rectangles that contain points with distance at least ${r}_{H}$ from $t$ . This is described by Algorithm 7 as a primitive. For every $h = {r}_{H},\ldots ,d$ ,we construct the set of rectangles ${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$ such that if $p$ belongs to a rectangle in ${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$ then ${\phi }_{H}\left( {p,t}\right)  = h$ . In particular,for every subset $X \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ with $\left| X\right|  = h$ ,we compute a set of intervals that will be used to create the rectangles in ${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$ . If ${A}_{j} \notin  X$ ,let ${I}_{j} = \left\lbrack  {t \cdot  {A}_{j} - \gamma ,t \cdot  {A}_{j} + \gamma }\right\rbrack$ ; otherwise,let ${I}_{j}^{ - } = \left( {-\infty ,t.{A}_{j} - \gamma \rbrack \text{and}{I}_{j}^{ + } = \left\lbrack  {t.{A}_{j} + \gamma , + \infty }\right) \text{and let}{\mathcal{R}}_{X}^{t} = \left( {{ \times  }_{{A}_{j} \notin  X}{I}_{j}}\right)  \times  \left( {{ \times  }_{{A}_{j} \in  X}\left\{  {{I}_{j}^{ - },{I}_{j}^{ + }}\right\}  }\right. }\right)$ . We define ${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) } = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{X \subseteq  \mathbf{A},\left| X\right|  = h}}{\mathcal{R}}_{X}^{t}$ ,and let ${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) } = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{h = {r}_{H},\ldots ,d}}{\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$ . See an example in Figure 4. We add the set of rectangles ${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ in $\mathcal{R}$ ,update the decomposition $\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ ,and proceed with the next iteration. At the end of Algorithm 6 (line 13), we check whether there is any uncovered point, i.e., if a tuple in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ lies in a cell with density $k$ . If no,we return $S$ ; otherwise,we proceed with the next value of ${r}_{H}$ . Due to space limit,the analysis is shown in Appendix B.2.

由于我们无法确定最佳内聚度与${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \in  \{ 1,\ldots ,d\}$，因此尝试将每个${r}_{H} \in  \{ 1,2,\ldots ,d\}$值作为$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$的推测值。我们最多重复以下步骤$k$次迭代：在第$i$次迭代中，遍历$\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$中的每个单元格，直至找到密度为$i$的单元格（即恰好被$i$个矩形包含的单元格）。设$C$为满足$\left| {C \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right|  \geq  1$条件的密度$i$单元格，通过矩形预言机获取任意元组$t \in  C \cap  Q$(I)。若$i = 1,C$是包含$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$内所有元组的矩形，且$t$是$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的任意元组。接着构建${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$集合，包含与$t$距离至少为${r}_{H}$的点的$O\left( 1\right)$个矩形（如算法7所述的基础操作）。对每个$h = {r}_{H},\ldots ,d$，构建矩形集${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$使得：若$p$属于${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$中的矩形，则${\phi }_{H}\left( {p,t}\right)  = h$。具体而言，对每个含$\left| X\right|  = h$的子集$X \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$，计算用于生成${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$矩形的区间集。若${A}_{j} \notin  X$，令${I}_{j} = \left\lbrack  {t \cdot  {A}_{j} - \gamma ,t \cdot  {A}_{j} + \gamma }\right\rbrack$；否则令${I}_{j}^{ - } = \left( {-\infty ,t.{A}_{j} - \gamma \rbrack \text{and}{I}_{j}^{ + } = \left\lbrack  {t.{A}_{j} + \gamma , + \infty }\right) \text{and let}{\mathcal{R}}_{X}^{t} = \left( {{ \times  }_{{A}_{j} \notin  X}{I}_{j}}\right)  \times  \left( {{ \times  }_{{A}_{j} \in  X}\left\{  {{I}_{j}^{ - },{I}_{j}^{ + }}\right\}  }\right. }\right)$。定义${\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) } = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{X \subseteq  \mathbf{A},\left| X\right|  = h}}{\mathcal{R}}_{X}^{t}$，并设${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) } = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{h = {r}_{H},\ldots ,d}}{\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$（示例见图4）。将${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$矩形集加入$\mathcal{R}$，更新分解$\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$后继续下一轮迭代。算法6结束时（第13行），检查是否存在未被覆盖的点（即$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的元组是否位于密度$k$的单元格内）。若无则返回$S$，否则继续测试${r}_{H}$的下一个值。完整分析见附录B.2。

THEOREM 3.11. For an acyclic join $Q$ with $d$ attributes and a database I of input size $N$ ,a 2-cohesive $k$ -summary under Hamming metric can be computed in $O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$ space.

定理3.11. 对于具有$d$个属性的无环连接$Q$及输入规模为$N$的数据库I，可在$O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$时间内利用$O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$空间计算出汉明度量下的2-内聚$k$摘要。

## 4 Min-diverse Summaries

## 4 最小多样性摘要

In this section, we show how our ideas for constructing cohesive summaries can be used to design algorithms for constructing min-diverse summaries under the Euclidean and Hamming metrics.

本节将展示如何将构建内聚摘要的思想应用于设计算法，以在欧氏度量和汉明度量下构造最小多样性摘要。

### 4.1 Euclidean Metric

### 4.1 欧氏度量

It is known that an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for cohesive summaries is also an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for min-diverse summaries [64]. ${}^{7}$ After constructing an $\varepsilon$ -coreset ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ for cohesive summaries using the algorithm described in Section 3.1.3, we run the algorithm from [11], in the non-relational setting, to derive a min-diverse $k$ -summary over the set ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ . This algorithm returns a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -min-diverse $k$ -summary of ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ in $O\left( {\left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right| \log \left( \left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right| \right)  + k\left( {\log \left( \left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right| \right)  + {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right) }\right)$ time. Plugging Theorem 3.9 into this result,we obtain:

已知内聚摘要的$\varepsilon$-核心集同样适用于最小多样性摘要[64]。使用第3.1.3节所述算法构建内聚摘要的$\varepsilon$-核心集${P}_{\varepsilon }$后，我们在非关系型场景中运行文献[11]的算法，从集合${P}_{\varepsilon }$导出最小多样性$k$摘要。该算法可在$O\left( {\left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right| \log \left( \left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right| \right)  + k\left( {\log \left( \left| {P}_{\varepsilon }\right| \right)  + {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right) }\right)$时间内返回${P}_{\varepsilon }$的$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$-最小多样性$k$摘要。结合定理3.9可得：

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{7}$ A set ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ is an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for the min-diverse summary problem if the optimal min-diverse $k$ -summary in ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ is an $\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ -min diverse $k$ -summary in $Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ .

${}^{7}$ 当集合${P}_{\varepsilon }$中的最优最小多样性$k$摘要同时是$Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$中的$\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right)$-最小多样性$k$摘要时，该集合${P}_{\varepsilon }$即为最小多样性摘要问题的$\varepsilon$-核心集。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

THEOREM 4.1. For an acyclic join $Q$ of $d$ attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,and a parameter $\varepsilon  > 0$ ,a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -min-diverse $k$ -summary of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ under Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d},N{\log }^{2}N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}  }\right)$ time.

定理4.1 对于具有$d$个属性的无环连接$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，以及参数$\varepsilon  > 0$，可以在$O\left( {\min \left\{  {{k}^{2}N + {kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d},N{\log }^{2}N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d} + {k}^{\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1}}\right\}  }\right)$时间内计算出欧几里得度量下$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$-最小多样性$k$摘要。

As in Section 3.1.3, the space used by the algorithm in Theorem 4.1 depends on the algorithm for computing a $O\left( 1\right)$ -cohesive summary.

如第3.1.3节所述，定理4.1中算法使用的空间取决于计算$O\left( 1\right)$-内聚摘要的算法。

### 4.2 Hamming Metric

### 4.2 汉明度量

Again, our algorithm in Section 4.1 for the Euclidean metric cannot be applied to the Hamming metric because the coreset uses properties of the Euclidean metric. However, our algorithm in Section 3.2 for cohesive summaries under the Hamming metric can be extended to min-diverse summaries. Let ${\sigma }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ be the minimum pairwise distance of the optimum min-diverse summary on $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . For every new tuple $t$ we add to the returned set $S$ ,we compute a set of $O\left( 1\right)$ nonintersecting rectangles that contain tuples with distance at least ${\sigma }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) /2$ from $t$ . There are only two minor differences with Algorithm 6. i) In line 2,we search for ${r}_{h} \in  \{ \lceil d/2\rceil  + 1,\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1 - 1,\ldots ,1\}$ to find the largest distance that separates the selected tuples. ii) In line 13, instead of checking whether there is no uncovered tuple,we check whether $S$ has size $k$ . We obtain the next theorem.

同样地，由于核心集利用了欧几里得度量的特性，第4.1节中针对欧几里得度量的算法无法直接应用于汉明度量。不过，我们在第3.2节提出的汉明度量下内聚摘要算法可以扩展至最小多样性摘要。设${\sigma }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$为最优最小多样性摘要$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$上的最小成对距离。对于每个新加入返回集$S$的元组$t$，我们计算一组$O\left( 1\right)$个不相交矩形，这些矩形包含与$t$距离至少为${\sigma }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) /2$的元组。与算法6相比仅有两处细微差异：i) 在第2行中，我们搜索${r}_{h} \in  \{ \lceil d/2\rceil  + 1,\lceil d/2\rceil  + 1 - 1,\ldots ,1\}$以寻找分隔选定元组的最大距离；ii) 在第13行中，不再检查是否存在未被覆盖的元组，而是检查$S$的大小是否为$k$。由此得到下述定理。

THEOREM 4.2. For acyclic join $Q$ with $d$ attributes and a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,a $\frac{1}{2}$ -min-diverse $k$ -summary under Hamming metric can be computed in $O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$ space.

定理4.2 对于具有$d$个属性的无环连接$Q$，以及输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，可以在$O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$空间计算出汉明度量下的$\frac{1}{2}$-最小多样性$k$摘要。

## 5 Sum-diverse Summaries

## 5 和多样性摘要

We now describe the algorithms for constructing sum-diverse summaries under the Euclidean and Hamming metrics.

本节将阐述在欧几里得与汉明度量下构建和多样性摘要的算法。

### 5.1 Euclidean Metric

### 5.1 欧几里得度量

Main ideas. In the non-relational setting, the following iterative algorithm described in [45] returns an $\frac{1}{2}$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary over a set of points $P$ . In each of the $k/2$ iterations,compute the farthest pair $\left( {{p}_{1},{p}_{2}}\right)$ in $P$ ,add $\left\{  {{p}_{1},{p}_{2}}\right\}$ in the summary,remove them from $P$ ,and continue with the next iteration. To the best of our knowledge, there is no efficient algorithm to compute the farthest pair in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ (in the relational setting). Instead,we use the idea proposed in [11] to approximately compute the farthest pair among a set of points in the Euclidean setting using the notion of $\varepsilon$ -net.

核心思路。在非关系型场景中，文献[45]描述的迭代算法可返回点集$P$上的$\frac{1}{2}$-和多样性$k$摘要。该算法在$k/2$次迭代中，每次计算$P$中最远点对$\left( {{p}_{1},{p}_{2}}\right)$，将其$\left\{  {{p}_{1},{p}_{2}}\right\}$加入摘要后从$P$移除，并继续下一轮迭代。据我们所知，目前尚无高效算法能在关系型场景中计算$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的最远点对。为此，我们采用文献[11]提出的思路，利用$\varepsilon$-网概念来近似计算欧几里得场景中的最远点对。

Definition 5.1 (ε-net). Let ${\mathbb{S}}^{d - 1}$ be the unit sphere in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ . A centrally symmetric set $\mathcal{C} \subseteq  {\mathbb{S}}^{d - 1}$ (i.e., if $u \in  C$ ,then $- u \in  C$ ) of $r = O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}\right)$ unit vectors in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ is an $\varepsilon$ -net if for every point $v \in  {\mathbb{S}}^{d - 1}$ , there exists a point $u \in  \mathcal{C}$ with angle at most ${\cos }^{-1}\left( \frac{1}{1 + \varepsilon }\right)  = O\left( \sqrt{\varepsilon }\right)$ .

定义5.1（ε-网）。设${\mathbb{S}}^{d - 1}$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中的单位球面。若中心对称集$\mathcal{C} \subseteq  {\mathbb{S}}^{d - 1}$（即若$u \in  C$，则$- u \in  C$）包含${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中$r = O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}\right)$个单位向量，且对任意点$v \in  {\mathbb{S}}^{d - 1}$都存在某点$u \in  \mathcal{C}$使得两者夹角不超过${\cos }^{-1}\left( \frac{1}{1 + \varepsilon }\right)  = O\left( \sqrt{\varepsilon }\right)$，则该集合称为$\varepsilon$-网。

As shown in [7,22],for any pair of points $x,y \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,it holds $\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right) \phi \left( {x,y}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  C}}\langle u,x - y\rangle  \leq$ $\phi \left( {x,y}\right)$ . Hence,a $\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ -approximation of the maximum pairwise distance in a set of points can be found by only checking the top-1 points with respect to the vectors in the $\varepsilon$ -net. Agarwal et al. [11],select the top $k$ points in each vector $u \in  \mathcal{C}$ ,and then run the iterative algorithm [45] on the union of the selected (top $k$ ) points to return a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary.

如文献[7,22]所示，对任意点对$x,y \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$都满足$\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right) \phi \left( {x,y}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  C}}\langle u,x - y\rangle  \leq$$\phi \left( {x,y}\right)$。因此，通过仅检查$\varepsilon$-网中各向量对应的top-1点，即可找到点集中最大成对距离的$\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right)$近似值。Agarwal等人[11]选取每个向量$u \in  \mathcal{C}$方向上的前$k$个点，并在所选(top $k$)点的并集上运行迭代算法[45]，最终返回$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$和多样化的$k$摘要。

Our algorithm. We next describe our algorithm in the relational setting with the pseudocode given in Algorithm 8 and a running example in Figure 5. Let $\mathcal{C}$ be a centrally symmetric $\varepsilon$ -net. We compute the top $k$ tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ for every vector $u \in  \mathcal{C}$ using our relational top- $k$ oracle proposed in Section 2. More specifically,we define the weight function ${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$ for tuples in ${R}_{i}$ as ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{A}}_{i}}}\left( {p \cdot  {A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$ ,where $p \in  {R}_{i}$ . We construct the top- $k$ oracle by defining a ranked enumeration index ${Z}_{u}$ with the vector of weight functions $\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},\ldots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$ . Let ${P}_{u}$ be the set of $k$ maximal tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ in direction $u$ ,enumerated by ${Z}_{u}$ in descending order of their weights. At last,we invoke the iterative algorithm [45] to find a sum-diverse $k$ -summary over $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$ .

我们的算法。接下来在关系型场景中描述算法，伪代码见算法8，运行示例见图5。设$\mathcal{C}$为中心对称的$\varepsilon$-网。我们使用第2节提出的关系型top-$k$查询器，计算每个向量$u \in  \mathcal{C}$在$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的前$k$个元组。具体而言，定义${R}_{i}$中元组的权重函数为${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{A}}_{i}}}\left( {p \cdot  {A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$（其中$p \in  {R}_{i}$），通过构建带权重函数向量$\overrightarrow{w} = \left\langle  {{w}_{1},\ldots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$的排序枚举索引${Z}_{u}$来实现top-$k$查询。令${P}_{u}$表示$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中沿$u$方向按权重降序排列的$k$个最大元组集合，最后调用迭代算法[45]在$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$上生成和多样化的$k$摘要。

The top-2 tuples with respect to vector $u \in  \mathcal{C}$ are ${t}_{1},{t}_{2}$ . The set $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$ contains the red points/tuples.

图5. 含8个向量的中心对称集$\mathcal{C}$。各点代表$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的元组。假设$k = 2$，向量$u \in  \mathcal{C}$对应的top-2元组为${t}_{1},{t}_{2}$。集合$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$包含图中红色点/元组。

Algorithm 8: GeometricSUMDiverse(Q,I,k)

算法8：几何和多样性摘要(Q,I,k)

---

$\mathcal{C} \leftarrow$ a centrally symmetric $\varepsilon$ -net;

$\mathcal{C} \leftarrow$ 一个中心对称的$\varepsilon$网络；

foreach $u \in  C$ do

对每个$u \in  C$执行

	foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ do

	对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$执行

		foreach $p \in  {R}_{i}$ do

		对每个$p \in  {R}_{i}$执行

			${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}}\left( {p \cdot  {A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$

	$\overrightarrow{w} \leftarrow  \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle  ;$

	${Z}_{u} \leftarrow$ an index built for $Q,\mathrm{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ as Lemma 2.1;

	${Z}_{u} \leftarrow$ 为$Q,\mathrm{I},\overrightarrow{w}$构建如引理2.1所述的索引；

	${P}_{u} \leftarrow$ the first $k$ results enumerated from ${Z}_{u}$ ;

	${P}_{u} \leftarrow$ 从${Z}_{u}$枚举的前$k$个结果；

$S \leftarrow$ sum-diverse $k$ -summary over $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$ [45];

$S \leftarrow$ 在$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$上的和多样性$k$摘要[45]；

return $S$ ;

返回$S$；

---

<!-- Media -->

Correctness. By the definition of the top- $k$ oracle (Lemma 2.2),the next lemma holds. For completeness, we also show the straightforward proof in Appendix C.

正确性。根据前$k$预言机的定义（引理2.2），下述引理成立。为完备性，我们在附录C中展示了直接证明。

LEMMA 5.2. For $\overrightarrow{w}$ at line 6 of Algorithm $8,\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = \langle u,t\rangle$ for every tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

引理5.2。对于算法$8,\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = \langle u,t\rangle$第6行的$\overrightarrow{w}$，每个元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$都成立。

By Lemma 5.2,for any vector $u \in  \mathcal{C}$ ,all tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be enumerated in a decreasing ordering of their inner product with $u$ . The correctness follows from [11] and the discussion above.

根据引理5.2，对于任意向量$u \in  \mathcal{C}$，$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的所有元组都能按其与$u$内积的降序枚举。正确性源自[11]及上述讨论。

Complexity. The $\varepsilon$ -net $C$ can be computed in $O\left( r\right)$ time [6]. For each vector $u \in  \mathcal{C}$ ,we construct an index ${Z}_{u}$ in $O\left( N\right)$ time. In total,we can construct $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$ in $O\left( {r\left( {N + k\log N}\right) }\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + {rk}}\right)$ space. Finally,the algorithm in [45] over the set $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$ (as implemented in [11]) runs in $O\left( {{rk}\log N}\right)$ time. The algorithm uses $O\left( {rk}\right)$ space to store the top $k$ tuples for every vector in $C$ .

复杂度。$C$网络$\varepsilon$可在$O\left( r\right)$时间内计算[6]。对每个向量$u \in  \mathcal{C}$，我们在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构建索引${Z}_{u}$。总体上，我们可用$O\left( {N + {rk}}\right)$空间在$O\left( {r\left( {N + k\log N}\right) }\right)$时间内构建$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$。最后，基于集合$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  C}}{P}_{u}$的[45]算法（如[11]实现）运行时间为$O\left( {{rk}\log N}\right)$。该算法使用$O\left( {rk}\right)$空间存储$C$中每个向量的前$k$个元组。

THEOREM 5.3. For an acyclic join $Q$ of $d$ attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,and a parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,\frac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary of $Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ under Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {\left( {N + k \cdot  \log N}\right) {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k{\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$ space.

定理5.3。对于具有$d$属性的无环连接$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，及参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,\frac{1}{2}}\right)$，可在$O\left( {\left( {N + k \cdot  \log N}\right) {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + k{\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$空间计算出欧氏度量下$Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$的$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$和多样性$k$摘要。

### 5.2 Hamming Metric

### 5.2 汉明度量

For the Hamming metric, we propose two algorithms that construct sum-diverse summaries. The first computes a better sum-diverse $k$ -summary,while the second is faster by a factor of $k$ .

针对汉明度量，我们提出两种构建和多样性摘要的算法。第一种生成更优的和多样性$k$摘要，第二种速度提升$k$倍。

Main ideas. In [21], the authors showed that if a distance is of negative type [62, 63], then a local search algorithm returns a $\left( {1 - 2/k}\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary in the non-relational setting.

核心观点。文献[21]中，作者证明若某距离满足负型(negative type)[62,63]条件，则局部搜索算法在非关系型场景下可返回一个$\left( {1 - 2/k}\right)$-和多样性$k$-摘要。

Definition 5.4 (Negative Type). Let $\mathcal{D} \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{n \times  n}$ be the distance matrix of distance function $\sigma$ . The function $\sigma$ is of negative type if for any vector $x = \left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{n}}\right)$ with $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{x}_{i} = 0,{x}^{\top }\mathcal{D}x \leq  0$ .

定义5.4(负型)。设$\mathcal{D} \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{n \times  n}$为距离函数$\sigma$的距离矩阵。当对任意满足$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{x}_{i} = 0,{x}^{\top }\mathcal{D}x \leq  0$的向量$x = \left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{n}}\right)$时，该函数$\sigma$属于负型。

In Appendix C, we prove that the Hamming distance is of negative type. Hence, we can obtain a relational version of the local search algorithm. Intuitively,our algorithm starts with a set $S$ of $k$ arbitrary tuples from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and then repeats the following step for at most $O\left( {k\log k}\right)$ iterations: if there exists a pair of tuples $x \in  S$ and $y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ such that replacing $x$ with $y$ in $S$ can increase its diversity,i.e., $\delta \left( {S\cup \{ y\} \smallsetminus \{ x\} }\right)  > \delta \left( S\right)$ ,we update $S$ accordingly; Otherwise,we just return $S$ .

附录C中，我们证明了汉明距离(Hamming distance)属于负型。因此可获得局部搜索算法的关系型版本。直观而言，该算法从$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中选取$k$个任意元组构成初始集合$S$，随后最多进行$O\left( {k\log k}\right)$次迭代：若存在元组对$x \in  S$与$y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$使得用$y$替换$S$中的$x$能提升多样性(即$\delta \left( {S\cup \{ y\} \smallsetminus \{ x\} }\right)  > \delta \left( S\right)$)，则更新$S$；否则直接返回$S$。

Our algorithm. In the relational setting,it is challenging to find the tuples $x,y$ to update the set $S$ . We next describe our algorithm in more detail with the pseudocode given in Algorithm 9. Initially,we add $k$ arbitrary tuples from $Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ to $S$ . For every tuple $t \in  S$ ,we define its diversity as $u\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x \in  S\smallsetminus \{ t\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {x,t}\right)$ ,i.e.,its sum of distances with remaining tuples in $S$ . Note that the diversity of $S$ is essentially $\Delta  = \frac{1}{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S}}u\left( t\right)$ . We repeat the following step for at most $O\left( {k\log k}\right)$ iterations. ${}^{8}$ We call Algorithm 10 as a primitive to compute the pair of tuples ${p}^{ - } \in  S,{p}^{ + } \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ such that the diversity $M = \delta \left( {S \cup  \left\{  {p}^{ + }\right\}   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}  }\right)$ is maximized. We do it as follows. For each tuple $x \in  S$ ,we construct an index $Z$ from Lemma 2.1 with the following weight function $\overrightarrow{w}$ . More specifically,for every tuple $p \in  {R}_{i}$ ,we define the weight function ${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$ as ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},p.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)$ . All tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ will be enumerated from $Z$ in the descending ordering to their sum of distances with tuples in $S \smallsetminus  \{ x\}$ ,until we encounter some tuple $y \notin  S$ . In Algorithm 10 we use the notation ${\Delta }_{x,y}$ to maintain the diversity of the set $S \cup  \{ y\}  \smallsetminus  \{ x\}$ . If replacing ${p}^{ - }$ by ${p}^{ + }$ in $S$ does not increase the diversity (line 7 of Algorithm 9) we stop and return $S$ . Otherwise,we replace ${p}^{ - }$ by ${p}^{ + }$ in $S$ and update $u\left( t\right)$ for every $t \in  S \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}$ . Then,we enter into the next iteration.

我们的算法。在关系型设定中，寻找待更新的元组$x,y$以更新集合$S$具有挑战性。接下来我们将通过算法9中的伪代码更详细地描述该算法。初始阶段，我们从$Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$中随机选取$k$个元组加入$S$。对于每个元组$t \in  S$，我们将其多样性定义为$u\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x \in  S\smallsetminus \{ t\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {x,t}\right)$，即该元组与$S$中其余元组的距离之和。需注意$S$的多样性本质上是$\Delta  = \frac{1}{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S}}u\left( t\right)$。我们最多重复以下步骤$O\left( {k\log k}\right)$次迭代。${}^{8}$调用算法10作为基础方法，计算能使多样性$M = \delta \left( {S \cup  \left\{  {p}^{ + }\right\}   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}  }\right)$最大化的元组对${p}^{ - } \in  S,{p}^{ + } \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$。具体实现方式如下：为每个元组$x \in  S$，根据引理2.1构建索引$Z$，并采用以下权重函数$\overrightarrow{w}$。更准确地说，对于每个元组$p \in  {R}_{i}$，我们将权重函数${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$定义为${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},p.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)$。$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的所有元组将按照与$S \smallsetminus  \{ x\}$中元组的距离之和降序从$Z$中枚举，直至遇到某个元组$y \notin  S$。算法10中使用符号${\Delta }_{x,y}$来维护集合<b21></b1>的多样性。若在$S$中用${p}^{ + }$替换${p}^{ - }$未能提升多样性（算法9第7行），则终止并返回$S$；否则执行替换操作，并为每个$t \in  S \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}$更新$u\left( t\right)$，随后进入下一次迭代。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 9: LocalSEARCH(Q,I,k)

算法9：局部搜索(Q,I,k)

---

$S \leftarrow  k$ arbitrary tuples from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ;

$S \leftarrow  k$ 从$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中任取元组；

foreach $t \in  S$ do

遍历$t \in  S$执行

		$u\left( t\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x \in  S\smallsetminus \{ t\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {x,t}\right) ;$

$\Delta  \leftarrow  \frac{1}{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S}}u\left( t\right) ;$

for $j \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,O\left( {k\log k}\right) \}$ do

对于$j \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,O\left( {k\log k}\right) \}$执行

			$\left( {{p}^{ - },{p}^{ + },M}\right)  \leftarrow  \operatorname{RepLACE}\left( {Q,\mathbf{I},S}\right)$ ;

			if $\Delta  > M$ then return $S$ ;

					若$\Delta  > M$则返回$S$；

			foreach $t \in  S \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}$ do

					遍历$t \in  S \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}$执行

						$u\left( t\right)  \leftarrow$

							$u\left( t\right)  - {\phi }_{H}\left( {t,{p}^{ - }}\right)  + {\phi }_{H}\left( {t,{p}^{ + }}\right) ;$

			$u\left( {p}^{ + }\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}  }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,{p}^{ + }}\right) ;$

			$S \leftarrow  S \cup  \left\{  {p}^{ + }\right\}   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {p}^{ - }\right\}$

			$\Delta  \leftarrow  \frac{1}{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S}}u\left( t\right)$ ;

return $S$ ;

返回$S$；

---

Algorithm 10: RepLACE(Q,I,S)

算法10：替换(Q,I,S)

---

$M \leftarrow   - \infty ,{p}^{ - } \leftarrow$ null, ${p}^{ + } \leftarrow$ null;

$M \leftarrow   - \infty ,{p}^{ - } \leftarrow$空值，${p}^{ + } \leftarrow$空值；

foreach $x \in  S$ do

遍历$x \in  S$执行

	foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ do

	遍历$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$执行

		foreach $p \in  {R}_{i}$ do

		遍历$p \in  {R}_{i}$执行

			${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  \leftarrow$

				$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\backslash \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},p.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)$ ;

	$\overrightarrow{w} \leftarrow  \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle  ;$

	$Z \leftarrow$ index built for $Q,\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ as Lemma 2.1;

	$Z \leftarrow$ 根据引理2.1为$Q,\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$建立索引；

	while true do

	循环执行

		$y \leftarrow$ a result enumerated from $Z$ ;

		$y \leftarrow$ 从$Z$枚举出的结果；

		if $y \notin  S$ then break;

		若$y \notin  S$则中断；

	${\Delta }_{x,y} \leftarrow  \Delta  - u\left( x\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {y.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\right) ;$

	if ${\Delta }_{x,y} > M$ then

	如果 ${\Delta }_{x,y} > M$ 则

		$M \leftarrow  {\Delta }_{x,y},\left( {{p}^{ - },{p}^{ + }}\right)  \leftarrow  \left( {x,y}\right)$ ;

return $\left( {{p}^{ - },{p}^{ + },M}\right)$ ;

返回 $\left( {{p}^{ - },{p}^{ + },M}\right)$ ;

---

<!-- Media -->

Finally,in order to compute ${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$ efficiently,we build a binary search tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$ ,initially empty,for every attribute ${A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}$ ,as follows. For every $y \in  S \smallsetminus  \{ x\}$ ,we check whether the value $y.{A}_{j}$ exists in ${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$ . If not,we add a node $u$ to ${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$ with value $u$ .value $= y.{A}_{j}$ along with a counter $u$ .count $= 1$ . If yes,then let $u$ be the node with $u$ .value $= y.{A}_{j}$ . We increase the counter $u$ .count by 1 . After constructing ${T}_{j}$ ,for every ${A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}$ ,we visit every tuple $p \in  {R}_{i}$ and we search each ${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$ with key $p.{A}_{j}$ . Let ${u}_{j}$ be the node such that ${u}_{j}$ .value $= p.{A}_{j}$ . We compute ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)$ as $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}}\left( {\left| S\right|  - {u}_{j}\text{.count}}\right)$ .

最后，为高效计算${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$，我们为每个属性${A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}$构建初始为空的二叉搜索树${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$，步骤如下：对于每个$y \in  S \smallsetminus  \{ x\}$，检查值$y.{A}_{j}$是否存在于${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$中。若不存在，则向${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$添加节点$u$，其值$u$.value$= y.{A}_{j}$及计数器$u$.count$= 1$；若存在，则令$u$为对应值$u$.value$= y.{A}_{j}$的节点，并将计数器$u$.count增加1。构建完${T}_{j}$后，对于每个${A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}$，遍历每个元组$p \in  {R}_{i}$，并以键$p.{A}_{j}$搜索各${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$。设${u}_{j}$为满足${u}_{j}$.value$= p.{A}_{j}$的节点，计算${w}_{i}\left( p\right)$为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}}\left( {\left| S\right|  - {u}_{j}\text{.count}}\right)$。

Correctness. We prove the next lemma.

正确性证明。我们证明以下引理。

LEMMA 5.5. For $\overrightarrow{w}$ at line 6 of Algorithm 10, $\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,y}\right)$ for every tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

引理5.5。对于算法10第6行的$\overrightarrow{w}$，每个元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$满足$\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,y}\right)$。

Proof. For $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathrm{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,y}\right)$ .

证明。对于$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathrm{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,y}\right)$。

For $x \in  S$ ,by Lemma 5.5,tuple $y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ that maximizes $\delta \left( {S\cup \{ y\} \smallsetminus \{ x\} }\right)$ can always be correctly found. Following [21] and the fact that Hamming distance is of negative type, our algorithm returns a $\left( {1 - \frac{2}{k}}\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary for $\mathcal{Q}\left( I\right)$ .

对于$x \in  S$，根据引理5.5，总能正确找到使$\delta \left( {S\cup \{ y\} \smallsetminus \{ x\} }\right)$最大化的元组$y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$。结合文献[21]及汉明距离的负型性质，本算法返回$\mathcal{Q}\left( I\right)$的$\left( {1 - \frac{2}{k}}\right)$和多样性$k$摘要。

Complexity. Initially, $k$ tuples from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be retrieved in $O\left( {N + k}\right)$ time. In every iteration, for every $x \in  S$ ,it takes $O\left( {N\log k}\right)  = O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ time to assign the weights ${w}_{i}$ using the binary search trees ${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$ . The index $Z$ is constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time. Each tuple $y$ can be enumerated with $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ delay. In the worst case,it may skip at most $k$ tuples before finding one that does not belong to $S$ . Overall,for each tuple $x$ ,we spend $O\left( {N\log N + k\log N}\right)$ time.

复杂度分析。初始阶段，可在$O\left( {N + k}\right)$时间内从$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中检索$k$个元组。每次迭代中，对每个$x \in  S$，通过二叉搜索树${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$分配权重${w}_{i}$需消耗$O\left( {N\log k}\right)  = O\left( {N\log N}\right)$时间。索引$Z$的构建时间为$O\left( N\right)$。每个元组$y$的枚举延迟为$O\left( {\log N}\right)$。最坏情况下，可能跳过最多$k$个元组才能找到不属于$S$的元组。总体而言，处理每个元组$x$的时间成本为$O\left( {N\log N + k\log N}\right)$。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{8}$ The exact number of iterations is shown in the proof of Corollary 2 in [21].

${}^{8}$ 具体迭代次数参见文献[21]推论2的证明。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

THEOREM 5.6. For an acyclic join $Q$ and database I of input size $N$ ,a $\left( {1 - \frac{2}{k}}\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary under Hamming metric can be computed in $O\left( {N{k}^{2}{\log }^{2}N + {k}^{3}{\log }^{2}N}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k}\right)$ space.

定理5.6。对于无环连接$Q$和输入规模为$N$的数据库I，在汉明度量下可在$O\left( {N{k}^{2}{\log }^{2}N + {k}^{3}{\log }^{2}N}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + k}\right)$空间计算出$\left( {1 - \frac{2}{k}}\right)$-和多样化$k$-摘要。

Remark 1. The Euclidean metric is also of negative type. However, due to the square root operations in the computation of the Euclidean metric, we cannot use the ranked enumeration index to get the best tuple that improves the diversity in $S \smallsetminus  \{ t\}$ ,as we did in the Hamming metric. So,this algorithm does not apply to the Euclidean metric.

注1。欧氏度量也属于负型度量。但由于欧氏度量计算涉及平方根运算，无法像汉明度量那样通过排序枚举索引获取最佳元组$S \smallsetminus  \{ t\}$来提升多样性。因此本算法不适用于欧氏度量。

Remark 2. The same high-level idea can be extended to another faster algorithm, but its quality is slightly worse than Theorem 5.6. Intuitively,we begin with a set $S$ containing one arbitrary tuple from $Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ ,and apply the following greedy strategy for $k$ iterations. In each iteration,we find the tuple $y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ that maximizes the sum of all pairwise distances with tuples in $S$ , i.e.,arg $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {p,t}\right)$ ,and add it to $S$ . This greedy approach returns a $\frac{1}{2}$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Note that the problem of finding the tuple from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ with the maximum sum of distances from tuples in $S$ is similar to finding the best tuple $y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ to replace a tuple $x \in  S$ as we had in the local search algorithm above. Due to the space limit,all details can be found in Appendix C. Hence, using the same machinery from our previous algorithm, we have:

注2。相同核心思想可扩展为更快速算法，但其质量略逊于定理5.6。简言之，我们从$Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$中任取一元组构建初始集合$S$，进行$k$次贪婪迭代：每次寻找能使集合内所有成对距离之和最大化的元组$y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$（即arg$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {p,t}\right)$）加入集合。该贪婪算法可为$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$生成$\frac{1}{2}$-和多样化$k$-摘要。需注意，从$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$中寻找与集合$S$距离之和最大的元组，其本质与前述局部搜索算法中替换元组$x \in  S$的最佳元组$y \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)  \smallsetminus  S$选择问题类似。因篇幅限制，详见附录C。基于原有算法框架可得：

THEOREM 5.7. For an acyclic join $Q$ and a database I of input size $N$ ,a $\frac{1}{2}$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary under Hamming metric can be computed in $O\left( {\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{2}}\right) \log N}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k}\right)$ space.

定理5.7。对于无环连接$Q$和输入规模为$N$的数据库I，在汉明度量下可在$O\left( {\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{2}}\right) \log N}\right)$时间内使用$O\left( {N + k}\right)$空间计算出$\frac{1}{2}$-和多样化$k$-摘要。

## 6 Extensions

## 6 扩展应用

From acyclic joins to cyclic joins. All our algorithms can be extended to cyclic join queries by applying the generalized hypertree decomposition [42], as described in Appendix D. Each cyclic join is transformed into an acyclic one at the cost of increasing the input size from $N$ to ${N}^{\text{fhtw }}$ , where fhtw is defined as the fractional hypertree width of the input join query which roughly measures how close is the input query $Q$ from being acyclic (for example,for every acyclic query fhtw = 1). All our approximation algorithms derived for acyclic joins can be applied without any modification,but time complexity increases by replacing $N$ with ${N}^{\text{fhtw }}$ .

从无环连接到循环连接。通过应用广义超树分解[42]，我们所有的算法均可扩展至循环连接查询，具体方法如附录D所述。每个循环连接被转化为无环连接，代价是将输入规模从$N$增至${N}^{\text{fhtw }}$，其中fhtw（分数超树宽度）用于衡量输入查询$Q$接近无环的程度（例如所有无环查询的fhtw=1）。针对无环连接设计的近似算法可直接应用，但时间复杂度需将$N$替换为${N}^{\text{fhtw }}$。

From joins to join-project queries. All our algorithms can be extended to acyclic join-project queries using indexes for ranked enumeration over join-project queries [32]. Using the generalized hypertree decomposition [42], the results are also extended to cyclic join-project queries. All approximation ratios are preserved, but the time complexity for constructing sum-diverse summaries increases by a factor of $O\left( {\min \{ k,N\} }\right)$ ,since the index [32] for join-project queries can only support $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ -delay enumeration. The running time for constructing a cohesive or min-diverse summary remains the same for join-project queries. We show the details in Appendix E.

从连接到连接-投影查询。利用连接-投影查询的排序枚举索引[32]，我们所有算法均可扩展至无环连接-投影查询。通过广义超树分解[42]，结果亦可推广至循环连接-投影查询。所有近似比保持不变，但由于连接-投影查询索引[32]仅支持$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$延迟枚举，构建多样性摘要的时间复杂度增加$O\left( {\min \{ k,N\} }\right)$倍。对于连接-投影查询，构建内聚性或最小多样性摘要的运行时间保持不变。详见附录E。

## 7 Conclusion

## 7 结论

In this paper, we designed efficient algorithms for computing cohesive and diverse summaries for conjunctive query results under the Euclidean or Hamming metric. There are a few interesting questions left for future work. (1) General metric: In addition to Euclidean and Hamming metrics, it is unknown how to compute representative summaries for conjunctive query results under general metrics. (2) Broader quality functions: beyond cohesion and diversity, it remains to investigate a general framework of computing good summaries under various quality functions. (3) Dynamic setting: It is unknown how to maintain representative summaries for conjunctive query results in the dynamic settings, where input tuples can be inserted or deleted.

本文设计了在欧几里得或汉明度量下计算连接查询结果的内聚性与多样性摘要的高效算法。未来工作尚存若干开放问题：（1）通用度量：除欧几里得与汉明度量外，如何计算通用度量下的代表性摘要尚属未知；（2）更广质量函数：除内聚性与多样性外，需探索多种质量函数下计算优质摘要的通用框架；（3）动态场景：在支持元组增删的动态环境中，如何维护连接查询结果的代表性摘要仍有待研究。

[67] Z. Zhao, F. Li, and Y. Liu. Efficient join synopsis maintenance for data warehouse. In Proceedings of the 2020 ACM SIGMOD International Conference on Management of Data, pages 2027-2042, 2020.

## A Missing details from Section 1

## A 第1节补充材料

Dangling Tuples. Given an acyclic join $Q$ and a database I of input size $N$ ,we give the classic Yannakakis algorithm [65] that can remove dangling tuples that do not participate in any join result of $\mathcal{Q}\left( I\right)$ . This primitive runs in $O\left( N\right)$ time. For a CQ $Q$ and a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ ,all dangling tuples can be removed in $O\left( {N}^{\text{fhtw }}\right)$ time (Appendix D).

悬空元组。给定无环连接$Q$与输入规模为$N$的数据库I，采用经典Yannakakis算法[65]可移除不参与$\mathcal{Q}\left( I\right)$任何连接结果的悬空元组。该基础操作耗时$O\left( N\right)$。对于CQ$Q$与输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，所有悬空元组可在$O\left( {N}^{\text{fhtw }}\right)$时间内清除（附录D）。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 11: RemoveDangling(Q,I)

算法11：RemoveDangling(Q,I)

---

Let $\mathcal{T}$ be an arbitrary join tree of $Q$ with root $r$ ;

设$\mathcal{T}$为$Q$的任意以$r$为根的连接树；

while visit nodes a bottom-up way (excluding $r$ ) do

以自底向上方式遍历节点（不含$r$）时：

	foreach node $u$ visited do

	对每个访问节点$u$执行：

		${R}_{{p}_{u}} \leftarrow  {R}_{{p}_{u}} \ltimes  {R}_{u}$ for the parent node ${p}_{u}$ of $u$ ;

		为$u$的父节点${p}_{u}$执行${R}_{{p}_{u}} \leftarrow  {R}_{{p}_{u}} \ltimes  {R}_{u}$；

while visit nodes a top-down way (excluding leaves) do

以自顶向下方式遍历节点（不含叶节点）时：

	foreach node $u$ visited do

	对每个访问节点$u$执行：

		${R}_{{u}^{\prime }} \leftarrow  {R}_{{u}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{u}$ for each child node ${u}^{\prime }$ of $u$ ;

		为$u$的每个子节点${u}^{\prime }$执行${R}_{{u}^{\prime }} \leftarrow  {R}_{{u}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{u}$；

return updated I;

返回更新后的I；

---

<!-- Media -->

Reduction from plain data. The hardness of the problems defined on plain data points can be carried to a relational data setting via the following reduction. Suppose we are given a set $P$ of $n$ points in the $d$ -dimensional space,where $d = \left| \mathbf{A}\right|$ ,and each point $p \in  P$ is associated with $d$ values (coordinates) $\left\langle  {{p}_{1},{p}_{2},\cdots ,{p}_{d}}\right\rangle$ . Moreover, we give a distinct label ${p}_{\text{id }}$ to every point $p \in  P$ . We also label the attributes in $\mathbf{A}$ ,as ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{d}$ . We construct database I as follows.For every point $p \in  P$ ,we add a tuple ${t}_{i}^{p}$ to ${R}_{i}$ for every relation ${R}_{i}$ ,where ${t}_{i}^{p} \cdot  {A}_{j} = \left( {{p}_{\mathrm{{id}}},{p}_{j}}\right)$ for every ${A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$ . It can be easily checked that there is a one-to-one mapping between the query results in the join $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and points in $P$ . Let ${t}_{p},{t}_{{p}^{\prime }} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ be the query results corresponding to $p,{p}^{\prime } \in  P$ respectively. The distance between $p$ and ${p}^{\prime }$ is transformed to the distance between ${t}_{p}$ and ${t}_{{p}^{\prime }}$ . This reduction implies the NP-hardness of computing cohesive and min-diverse summaries.

从原始数据归约。定义在原始数据点上的问题难度可通过以下归约方法转移到关系型数据场景中。假设给定一个包含$P$个$n$维空间点的集合$d$，其中$d = \left| \mathbf{A}\right|$，且每个点$p \in  P$关联$d$个值（坐标）$\left\langle  {{p}_{1},{p}_{2},\cdots ,{p}_{d}}\right\rangle$。此外，我们为每个点$p \in  P$分配唯一标签${p}_{\text{id }}$，并将$\mathbf{A}$中的属性标记为${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{d}$。按如下方式构建数据库I：对每个点$p \in  P$，在关系${R}_{i}$中添加元组${t}_{i}^{p}$，其中对每个${A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$有${t}_{i}^{p} \cdot  {A}_{j} = \left( {{p}_{\mathrm{{id}}},{p}_{j}}\right)$。可验证连接查询$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的结果与集合$P$中的点存在一一映射关系。令${t}_{p},{t}_{{p}^{\prime }} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$分别对应$p,{p}^{\prime } \in  P$的查询结果，$p$与${p}^{\prime }$间的距离转化为${t}_{p}$与${t}_{{p}^{\prime }}$间的距离。该归约证明了计算内聚性和最小多样性摘要的NP难度。

Optimality. All lower bounds from non-relational settings hold in our relational setting. First, the dependency on $N$ in all our algorithms for acyclic join queries is near linear,which is optimal. Any algorithm for computing summaries needs to read the entire database at least. For Euclidean cohesive summary,we give $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -approximation algorithms in $\widetilde{O}\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$ or $\widetilde{O}\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{d}}\right)$ time (assuming $\varepsilon$ as a small constant). For the non-relational setting,the best algorithm for 2-cohesive summary under any general metric runs in $O\left( {Nk}\right)$ time. Hence,the approximation factor and complexities of our algorithms are close (by,at most,a factor of $k$ ) to the optimum algorithms in the non-relational setting. Exactly the same results and lower bounds hold for min-diversity summaries. For Euclidean sum-diverse summaries,we give a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -approximation algorithm in $\widetilde{O}\left( {N + k}\right)$ time. For general distances in the non-relational setting,the best algorithm returns a $\frac{1}{2}$ -approximation in $O\left( {Nk}\right)$ time,while in the Euclidean metric,the best algorithm returns a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -approximation in $\widetilde{O}\left( {N + k}\right)$ time. Our algorithm for sum-diverse summaries in the Euclidean metric is optimal.

最优性。非关系型场景中的所有下界在我们的关系型场景中同样成立。首先，针对无环连接查询的所有算法对$N$的依赖接近线性，这是最优的。任何摘要计算算法至少需要读取整个数据库。对于欧氏内聚摘要，我们在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N{k}^{2}}\right)$或$\widetilde{O}\left( {{Nk} + {k}^{d}}\right)$时间内给出$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$-近似算法（假设$\varepsilon$为小常数）。在非关系型场景中，任意通用度量下2-内聚摘要的最佳算法运行时间为$O\left( {Nk}\right)$。因此，我们算法的近似因子和复杂度与非关系型场景中的最优算法相差最多$k$倍。完全相同的结果和下界适用于最小多样性摘要。对于欧氏和多样性摘要，我们在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N + k}\right)$时间内给出$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$-近似算法。在非关系型场景的通用度量中，最佳算法在$O\left( {Nk}\right)$时间内返回$\frac{1}{2}$-近似解，而在欧氏度量中，最佳算法在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N + k}\right)$时间内返回$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$-近似解。我们在欧氏度量中和多样性摘要的算法是最优的。

对于某一项目$b$及一组项目$A$，令$\operatorname{NN}\left( {b,A}\right)$表示$b$在$A$中的最近邻，即$\mathrm{{NN}}\left( {b,A}\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{a \in  A}}\phi \left( {b,a}\right)$。设${O}_{1}$为${\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的最优内聚$k$摘要，亦即$\rho \left( {{O}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  =$${\rho }_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。对于每个中心点$p \in  {O}_{1}$，令$\bar{p} = \mathrm{{NN}}\left( {p,{\bar{S}}_{u}}\right)$。根据引理3.3，可得$\phi \left( {p,\bar{p}}\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2}{r}^{ * }$。考虑$k$个中心点集合${O}_{2} = \left\{  {\bar{p} \mid  p \in  {O}_{1}}\right\}$。对于任意元组$x \in  {\bar{S}}_{u}$，令$\widehat{x} = \mathrm{{NN}}\left( {x,{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。由算法2可知$\phi \left( {x,\widehat{x}}\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2}{r}^{ * }$成立。因此，对于任意元组$s \in  {\bar{S}}_{u}$，若$o = \mathrm{{NN}}\left( {\widehat{s},{O}_{1}}\right)$，则有$\phi \left( {s,\bar{o}}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {s,\widehat{s}}\right)  + \phi \left( {\widehat{s},o}\right)  + \phi \left( {o,\bar{o}}\right)  \leq  \sqrt{2}{r}^{ * } + {\rho }_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  + \sqrt{2}{r}^{ * }$。由此可得${\rho }_{k}\left( {\bar{S}}_{u}\right)  \leq$${\rho }_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  + 2 \cdot  \sqrt{2}{r}^{ * }$。根据文献[38]的推论，$\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\bar{S}}_{u}}\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\bar{S}}_{u}\right)$。最终我们得出

$$

To show $\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \geq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) /2$ ,we resort to the generalized cohesive $k$ -summary problem by relaxing the condition that $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . 为证明$\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \geq  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) /2$，我们通过放宽$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的条件，将其转化为广义凝聚$k$摘要问题。设${\widetilde{\rho }}_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$为${Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的广义凝聚$k$摘要问题最优解。根据三角不等式，$\frac{1}{2} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\widetilde{\rho }}_{k}\left( {{Q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq$${\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。由定义可知，$\rho \left( {{S}_{u},{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \geq  {\widetilde{\rho }}_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \geq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  {\rho }_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。
