#### B.1.1 Coreset without having an upper bound on the clustering cost.

#### B.1.1 无聚类成本上界的核心集

Let $S$ be a $O\left( 1\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary. We do not know the value of $r$ ,however,we know that the approximation ratio is $\beta$ ,where $\beta$ is a constant. The high-level idea of our algorithm is as follows: We run a binary search over all possible ${L}_{\infty }$ distances in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . For a distance $\ell$ we check whether all tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be covered with balls having centers the points in $S$ and radius $\sqrt{d}\ell$ . In the end,we find a number $r$ such that ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  c \cdot  \beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ ,for a constant $c$ , and we execute the algorithm from Section 3.1.3. Let $A$ be a sorted array of the values among all the attributes in the database. We run a binary search on the pairwise distances of $A$ . In [60] the authors show that the $j$ -th smallest ${L}_{\infty }$ distance (or equivalently ${L}_{1}$ distance in ${\mathbb{R}}^{1}$ ) of $n$ points on a line can be computed in $O\left( {n\log n}\right)$ time. Let $\ell$ be a value we check in the binary search. Let $\widehat{\ell } = \sqrt{d}\ell$ . We define the grid ${G}_{\widehat{\ell }}$ having grid cells with diagonal ${\mu }_{\widehat{\ell }} = \varepsilon \widehat{\ell }$ . For each center $s \in  S$ ,we define the ball ${B}_{s}$ of radius $\widehat{\ell }$ and center $s$ . Let ${B}_{\widehat{\ell }} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{s \in  S}}{B}_{s}$ . We check whether ${B}_{\widehat{\ell }}$ covers all tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Unfortunately,we cannot visit all tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ and the complexity of constructing the union of $k$ balls is large. Instead,we visit each grid cell $g \in  {G}_{\widehat{\ell }}$ such that $g$ is contained or partially intersected by ${B}_{\widehat{\ell }}$ . Let ${\widehat{G}}_{\widehat{\ell }}$ be the set of these grid cells. For each $g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{\widehat{\ell }}$ we run a counting query using Lemma 2.3 to get ${f}_{g} = \left| {g \cap  Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right|$ . We also run an additional counting query in a rectangle that contains all tuples to find $f = \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right|$ . If $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{f}}}{f}_{g} = f$ then we continue the binary search with smaller values of $\ell$ . Otherwise,we continue with larger values of $\ell$ . Let ${\ell }^{ * }$ be the parameter in the last iteration of the binary search that ${\widehat{G}}_{{\ell }^{ * }}$ covered all tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . We set $r = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * }$ and then we follow the same procedure as in Subsection 3.1.3 to construct ${P}_{\varepsilon }$ .

设$S$为一个$O\left( 1\right)$内聚的$k$摘要。虽然我们不知道$r$的具体值，但已知近似比为$\beta$，其中$\beta$为常数。本算法的高层思路如下：我们对$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有可能的${L}_{\infty }$距离进行二分搜索。对于给定距离$\ell$，检查$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有元组能否被以$S$点为中心、$\sqrt{d}\ell$为半径的球体覆盖。最终，我们将找到满足${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  c \cdot  \beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的数值$r$（其中$c$为常数），并执行3.1.3节的算法流程。

令$A$为数据库中所有属性值的排序数组。我们对$A$元素间的成对距离实施二分搜索。文献[60]证明，直线上$n$个点的第$j$小${L}_{\infty }$距离（或等价于${\mathbb{R}}^{1}$中的${L}_{1}$距离）可在$O\left( {n\log n}\right)$时间内计算得出。

设$\ell$为二分搜索中的待检验值，令$\widehat{\ell } = \sqrt{d}\ell$。我们定义对角线长为${\mu }_{\widehat{\ell }} = \varepsilon \widehat{\ell }$的网格${G}_{\widehat{\ell }}$。对于每个中心点$s \in  S$，定义半径为$\widehat{\ell }$、中心为$s$的球体${B}_{s}$。令${B}_{\widehat{\ell }} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{s \in  S}}{B}_{s}$，我们验证${B}_{\widehat{\ell }}$是否能覆盖$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有元组。

由于无法遍历$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有元组，且构建$k$个球体并集的复杂度较高，我们改为访问每个被${B}_{\widehat{\ell }}$完全包含或部分相交的网格单元$g \in  {G}_{\widehat{\ell }}$。令${\widehat{G}}_{\widehat{\ell }}$表示这些网格单元的集合，对每个$g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{\widehat{\ell }}$使用引理2.3执行计数查询获取${f}_{g} = \left| {g \cap  Q\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right|$，同时在包含所有元组的矩形区域内执行额外计数查询以确定$f = \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right|$。若$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{f}}}{f}_{g} = f$成立，则以更小的$\ell$值继续二分搜索；反之则增大$\ell$值。

设${\ell }^{ * }$为二分搜索末次迭代中能覆盖$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$所有元组的参数，我们设定$r = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * }$后，按照3.1.3小节所述流程构建${P}_{\varepsilon }$。

Lemma B.1. ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}\beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ .

引理 B.1。${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}\beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。

Proof. For a vector $x \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,let $\parallel x{\parallel }_{\infty }$ be its ${L}_{\infty }$ norm,and let $\parallel x{\parallel }_{2}$ be its ${L}_{2}$ norm. By definition, for two vectors $x,y \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,we have $\parallel x - y{\parallel }_{2} = \phi \left( {x,y}\right)$ . It is known that for any vector $a \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ it holds, $\parallel a{\parallel }_{\infty } \leq  \parallel a{\parallel }_{2} \leq  \sqrt{d}\parallel a{\parallel }_{\infty }$ . For every tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,it is always true that there exists ${s}_{t} \in  S$ with ${\begin{Vmatrix}t - {s}_{t}\end{Vmatrix}}_{2} \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Let ${t}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ be the tuple such that ${\begin{Vmatrix}s - {s}_{{t}^{\prime }}\end{Vmatrix}}_{2} = \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Let $\ell  = {\begin{Vmatrix}s - {s}_{{t}^{\prime }}\end{Vmatrix}}_{\infty }$ . From above,it follows that ${\begin{Vmatrix}s - {s}_{{t}^{\prime }}\end{Vmatrix}}_{\infty } \geq  \frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( I\right) }\right) }{\sqrt{d}}$ . Hence,there exists an ${L}_{\infty }$ distance ${\ell }_{a}$ in $A$ satisfying ${\ell }_{a} \geq  \frac{\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) }{\sqrt{d}} \Leftrightarrow  \sqrt{d}{\ell }_{a} \geq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Hence,for any $\ell  \geq  {\ell }_{a}$ ,we have $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{\ell }}}{f}_{g} = f$ . So ${\ell }^{ * } \leq  {\ell }_{a}$ . Next,we show that ${\ell }^{ * } \geq  \frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( 1\right) }\right) }{\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}}$ by contradiction. Assume ${\ell }^{ * } < \frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( 1\right) }\right) }{\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}} \Leftrightarrow  \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * } <$ $\frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) }{1 + \varepsilon }$ . Our grid-based algorithm for checking whether all points in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ lie in the cells ${\widehat{G}}_{{\widehat{\ell }}^{ * }}$ , counts all tuples within distance $\sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * }$ from centers $S$ and might count some tuples within distance $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * }$ from centers in $S$ . Hence,if $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * } < \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ ,our algorithm will return $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{\ell }}}{f}_{g} < f$ . Overall, $\frac{1}{1 + \varepsilon } \cdot  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * } \leq  \sqrt{d}{\ell }_{a} \leq  \sqrt{d}\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{d}\beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$ .

证明。对于向量$x \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，设$\parallel x{\parallel }_{\infty }$为其${L}_{\infty }$范数，$\parallel x{\parallel }_{2}$为其${L}_{2}$范数。根据定义，对于两个向量$x,y \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，有$\parallel x - y{\parallel }_{2} = \phi \left( {x,y}\right)$。已知对于任意向量$a \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$都成立$\parallel a{\parallel }_{\infty } \leq  \parallel a{\parallel }_{2} \leq  \sqrt{d}\parallel a{\parallel }_{\infty }$。对于每个元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，总存在满足${\begin{Vmatrix}t - {s}_{t}\end{Vmatrix}}_{2} \leq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的${s}_{t} \in  S$。设${t}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$为使${\begin{Vmatrix}s - {s}_{{t}^{\prime }}\end{Vmatrix}}_{2} = \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$成立的元组。令$\ell  = {\begin{Vmatrix}s - {s}_{{t}^{\prime }}\end{Vmatrix}}_{\infty }$。由上可得${\begin{Vmatrix}s - {s}_{{t}^{\prime }}\end{Vmatrix}}_{\infty } \geq  \frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( I\right) }\right) }{\sqrt{d}}$。因此，在$A$中存在满足${\ell }_{a} \geq  \frac{\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) }{\sqrt{d}} \Leftrightarrow  \sqrt{d}{\ell }_{a} \geq  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$的${L}_{\infty }$距离${\ell }_{a}$。故对任意$\ell  \geq  {\ell }_{a}$，有$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{\ell }}}{f}_{g} = f$。所以${\ell }^{ * } \leq  {\ell }_{a}$。接下来用反证法证明${\ell }^{ * } \geq  \frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( 1\right) }\right) }{\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}}$。假设${\ell }^{ * } < \frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( 1\right) }\right) }{\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}} \Leftrightarrow  \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * } <$$\frac{\rho \left( {S,Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right) }{1 + \varepsilon }$。我们基于网格的算法在检查$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有点是否位于单元格${\widehat{G}}_{{\widehat{\ell }}^{ * }}$时，会统计距中心点$S$$\sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * }$范围内的所有元组，并可能包含距$S$中心点$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * }$范围内的部分元组。因此若$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * } < \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$，算法将返回$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{g \in  {\widehat{G}}_{\ell }}}{f}_{g} < f$。综上，$\frac{1}{1 + \varepsilon } \cdot  \rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{d}{\ell }^{ * } \leq  \sqrt{d}{\ell }_{a} \leq  \sqrt{d}\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{d}\beta {\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)$。

Correctness With Lemma B. 1 and algorithm in Section 3.1.3, we can construct a desired coreset. Complexity. For any $\ell$ ,there is at most $O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-d}\right)$ grid cells with diagonal $\varepsilon \sqrt{d}\ell$ that intersect or fully contained in a ball of radius $\sqrt{d}\ell$ . Hence,in each iteration of the binary search we spend $O\left( {{kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ to run the counting queries. Using [60] to get the $j$ -th smallest ${L}_{\infty }$ distance among $A$ , we need $O\left( {N{\log }^{2}N}\right)$ additional time to execute the binary search. Overall,given $S$ ,we construct an $\varepsilon$ -coreset in $O\left( {N{\log }^{2}\left( N\right)  + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time using $O\left( {N + k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ space.

正确性 根据引理B.1及第3.1.3节算法，可构建所需的核心集。复杂度分析 对于任意$\ell$，对角线长度为$\varepsilon \sqrt{d}\ell$且与半径为$\sqrt{d}\ell$的球体相交或完全包含其中的网格单元最多有$O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-d}\right)$个。因此，在二分搜索的每次迭代中，执行计数查询耗时$O\left( {{kN}{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$。采用文献[60]获取$A$个元素中第$j$小的${L}_{\infty }$距离，执行二分搜索还需额外$O\left( {N{\log }^{2}N}\right)$时间。综上，给定$S$时，我们可在$O\left( {N{\log }^{2}\left( N\right)  + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$时间内构建$\varepsilon$-核心集，空间复杂度为$O\left( {N + k{\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$。

### B.2 Missing details from Subsection 3.2

### B.2 第3.2节缺失细节

Correctness. By definition,for a fixed ${r}_{H}$ ,all rectangles in ${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ are disjoint and $p \in  {\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ if and only if ${\phi }_{H}\left( {t,p}\right)  \geq  {r}_{H}$ . Indeed,if $p \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$ ,for $h \geq  {r}_{H}$ ,then the tuple $p$ has different values than $t$ in exactly $h$ attributes. Furthermore,for a fixed ${r}_{H}$ ,the condition in line 13 is satisfied if and only if $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \geq  {r}_{H}$ . Indeed,a cell $\psi$ in $\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ has density $k$ if and only if every point $x \in  \psi$ has distance at least ${r}_{H}$ . Hence,if there is no tuple from $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ in these cells,then every tuple in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ is within distance ${r}_{H}$ from $S$ ,i.e., $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{H} - 1$ . We show that $\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . Equivalently,we show that for ${r}_{H} = 2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$ the condition in line 13 always holds. Let ${S}^{ * }$ be the optimally cohesive $k$ -summary of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . For every tuple ${t}_{j} \in  {S}^{ * }$ ,we define the ball ${\mathcal{B}}_{j}$ with center ${t}_{j}$ and radius ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . By definition,the union of all such $k$ balls covers all tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Let $t$ be the tuple that is selected in lines 10-11 of Algorithm 6 in an iteration $i$ . Without loss of generality assume that $t$ belongs in the ball ${\mathcal{B}}_{j}$ . The Hamming distance satisfies the triangle inequality,so for any $p \in  {\mathcal{B}}_{j} \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,it holds ${\phi }_{H}\left( {t,p}\right)  \leq  {\phi }_{H}\left( {t,{t}_{j}}\right)  + {\phi }_{H}\left( {{t}_{j},p}\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ . In other words,it holds that for any new tuple $t$ we add in $S$ ,the ball ${\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$ with center $t$ and radius $2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$ completely covers a ball from the optimally cohesive $k$ -summary. By definition,the union of the rectangles in ${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$ is the complement of ball ${\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$ . After $k$ iterations all optimal $k$ balls are covered by the balls $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$ ,so there is no tuple in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ that lies in the complement of $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$ . Equivalently, there is no tuple in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ that lies in a cell of density $k$ in line 13,so the condition is satisfied.

正确性。根据定义，对于固定的${r}_{H}$，${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$中所有矩形互不相交且$p \in  {\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$当且仅当${\phi }_{H}\left( {t,p}\right)  \geq  {r}_{H}$。事实上，若$p \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{h}^{\left( t\right) }$，对于$h \geq  {r}_{H}$而言，元组$p$与$t$恰好有$h$个属性值不同。此外，对于固定${r}_{H}$，第13行的条件满足当且仅当$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \geq  {r}_{H}$。具体而言，$\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$中的单元格$\psi$具有密度$k$当且仅当每个点$x \in  \psi$与最近邻距离至少为${r}_{H}$。因此，若这些单元格中不存在来自$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的元组，则$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中每个元组与$S$的距离均不超过${r}_{H}$，即$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{H} - 1$。我们证明$\rho \left( {S,\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。等价地，我们证明对于${r}_{H} = 2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right) }\right)  + 1$，第13行的条件恒成立。设${S}^{ * }$为$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的最优内聚$k$-摘要。对每个元组${t}_{j} \in  {S}^{ * }$，定义以${t}_{j}$为中心、${\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$为半径的球体${\mathcal{B}}_{j}$。根据定义，所有此类$k$球体的并集覆盖$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中全部元组。设$t$为算法6第10-11行在迭代$i$中选择的元组。不失一般性，假定$t$属于球体${\mathcal{B}}_{j}$。汉明距离满足三角不等式，故对任意$p \in  {\mathcal{B}}_{j} \cap  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，有${\phi }_{H}\left( {t,p}\right)  \leq  {\phi }_{H}\left( {t,{t}_{j}}\right)  + {\phi }_{H}\left( {{t}_{j},p}\right)  \leq  2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$。换言之，对于任何加入$S$的新元组$t$，以$t$为中心、$2{\rho }_{k}\left( {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }\right)$为半径的球体${\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$将完全覆盖最优内聚$k$-摘要中的某个球体。根据定义，${\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }$中矩形的并集是球体${\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$的补集。经过$k$次迭代后，所有最优$k$球体均被$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$覆盖，因此$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中不存在位于$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\mathcal{B}}_{t}^{\prime }$补集的元组。等价地，第13行中不存在位于密度为$k$单元格的$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$元组，故条件成立。

Complexity. The algorithm runs for $k$ iterations. In each iteration $i$ ,we add a tuple $t$ in $S$ and we construct $\left| {\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }\right|  = O\left( 1\right)$ rectangles. The decomposition $\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ is updated in ${k}^{d}$ time [10]. The cells with depth $i - 1$ can also be found in ${k}^{O\left( d\right) }$ time after updating the decomposition. We run a rectangular query for every cell of depth $i - 1$ . From Lemma 2.3,each rectangular query takes $O\left( N\right)$ time. Overall,our algorithm runs in $O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$ time and uses $O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$ space.

复杂度分析。该算法运行$k$次迭代。每次迭代$i$中，我们在$S$中添加元组$t$，并构建$\left| {\mathcal{R}}^{\left( t\right) }\right|  = O\left( 1\right)$个矩形。分解结构$\mathcal{M}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在${k}^{d}$时间内完成更新[10]。深度为$i - 1$的网格单元在更新分解后也能在${k}^{O\left( d\right) }$时间内被定位。我们对每个深度为$i - 1$的网格单元执行矩形查询。根据引理2.3，每次矩形查询耗时$O\left( N\right)$。综上，算法时间复杂度为$O\left( {N{k}^{d}}\right)$，空间复杂度为$O\left( {N + {k}^{d}}\right)$。

## C Missing details from Section 5

## C 第五章补充细节

\text{Proof of Lemma 5.2.}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}}\left( {t.{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}}}\left( {t.{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j} = \langle u,t\rangle \text{.}

LEMMA C.1. The Hamming metric is of negative type.

引理C.1. 汉明度量属于负类型。

Proof. Using [35,61],any distance function $\sigma$ ,over a set of $n$ items $P \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,is of negative type if there exists a mapping from $P$ to ${P}^{\prime } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{{d}^{\prime }}$ for a positive integer ${d}^{\prime }$ ,such that $\sigma \left( {p,q}\right)  = {\sigma }^{\prime }\left( {{p}^{\prime },{q}^{\prime }}\right)$ , where $p,q \in  P,{p}^{\prime }$ is the mapping of $p$ (similarly, ${q}^{\prime }$ is the mapping of $q$ ),and ${\sigma }^{\prime }$ is the squared Euclidean distance.

证明。依据[35,61]，对于$n$个元素$P \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$上的任意距离函数$\sigma$，若存在从$P$到某正整数${d}^{\prime }$维空间${P}^{\prime } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{{d}^{\prime }}$的映射，使得$\sigma \left( {p,q}\right)  = {\sigma }^{\prime }\left( {{p}^{\prime },{q}^{\prime }}\right)$成立（其中$p,q \in  P,{p}^{\prime }$表示$p$的映射，${q}^{\prime }$表示$q$的映射，${\sigma }^{\prime }$为欧氏距离平方），则该距离函数属于负类型。

For the Hamming metric,we map all points in $P \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ to points in ${d}^{\prime } \leq  n \cdot  {d}^{2}$ dimensions as follows. Let $L$ be the ordered list of all distinct values (coordinates) over the points in $P$ . Clearly, $\left| L\right|  \leq  {nd}$ . We use ${p}_{j}$ to denote the $j$ -th value of point $p \in  P$ . For every $p \in  P$ ,we create ${p}^{\prime } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| L\right| d}$ as follows. For each $j \in  \left\lbrack  d\right\rbrack$ ,we create the zero vector ${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| L\right| }$ ,such that ${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) } = \left( {0,\ldots ,0}\right)$ . Without loss of generality,assume that ${p}_{j} = L\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack$ ,i.e.,the $j$ -th value of $p$ is the $i$ -th element in list $L$ . We set ${\overrightarrow{X}}_{i}^{\left( j\right) } = 1/\sqrt{2}$ ,i.e.,the $i$ -th value of ${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) }$ is set to $1/\sqrt{2}$ . Then ${p}^{\prime }$ is the concatenation of all vectors ${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) }$ ,i.e., ${p}^{\prime } = \left\lbrack  {{\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( d\right) }}\right\rbrack$ . By definition,it holds that under the Hamming metric ${\phi }_{H}\left( {p,q}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  h \leq  d \mid  L \mid  }}{\left( {p}_{h}^{\prime } - {q}_{h}^{\prime }\right) }^{2}$ . Intuitively,if two points have different $j$ -th value then the squared Euclidean will sum up the terms ${\left( 1/\sqrt{2} - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( 0 - 1/\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} = 1$ .

对于汉明度量，我们将$P \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中的所有点映射到${d}^{\prime } \leq  n \cdot  {d}^{2}$维空间，具体方法如下。设$L$为$P$中所有不同值（坐标）的有序列表，显然$\left| L\right|  \leq  {nd}$。用${p}_{j}$表示点$p \in  P$的第$j$个值。对每个$p \in  P$，按以下方式创建${p}^{\prime } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| L\right| d}$：对于每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  d\right\rbrack$，创建零向量${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) } \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| L\right| }$，使得${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) } = \left( {0,\ldots ,0}\right)$。不失一般性，假设${p}_{j} = L\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack$，即$p$的第$j$个值是列表$L$中的第$i$个元素。我们设置${\overrightarrow{X}}_{i}^{\left( j\right) } = 1/\sqrt{2}$，即将${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) }$的第$i$个值设为$1/\sqrt{2}$。最终<b18></b8>是所有向量${\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( j\right) }$的串联，即${p}^{\prime } = \left\lbrack  {{\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{X}}^{\left( d\right) }}\right\rbrack$。根据定义，在汉明度量下满足${\phi }_{H}\left( {p,q}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  h \leq  d \mid  L \mid  }}{\left( {p}_{h}^{\prime } - {q}_{h}^{\prime }\right) }^{2}$。直观而言，若两点第$j$个值不同，则欧氏距离平方将累加项${\left( 1/\sqrt{2} - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( 0 - 1/\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} = 1$。

Greedy algorithm for Sum-Diverse Summaries. In Algorithm 12, we start with an arbitrary tuple ${x}_{0} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ and add it to $S$ . We repeat the following step until the size of $S$ reaches $k$ . We construct an index $Z$ that enumerates the tuples in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ in descending order to their sum of distances with the tuples in $S$ . More specifically,for every tuple $p \in  {R}_{i}$ ,we define the weight ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {p.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)$ . All tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ are enumerated from $Z$ in the descending ordering, until we encounter some result $y \notin  S$ . We add $y$ to $S$ and continue in the next iteration.

求和多样性摘要的贪心算法。在算法12中，我们从任意元组${x}_{0} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$开始，将其加入$S$。重复以下步骤直至$S$大小达到$k$：构建索引$Z$，按元组与$S$中元组的距离总和降序枚举$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的元组。具体地，对每个元组$p \in  {R}_{i}$定义权重${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {p.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)$，所有$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中的元组按$Z$降序排列，直至找到结果$y \notin  S$。将$y$加入$S$后继续下一轮迭代。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 12: GreedySUMDIVERSE(Q,I,k)

算法12：贪心求和多样性(GreedySUMDIVERSE)(Q,I,k)

---

$S \leftarrow  \left\{  {x}_{0}\right\}$ for an arbitrary tuple ${x}_{0} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ;

$S \leftarrow  \left\{  {x}_{0}\right\}$ 对于任意元组 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ；

while $\left| S\right|  \leq  k$ do

当 $\left| S\right|  \leq  k$ 时执行

	foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ do

	遍历 $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ 执行

		foreach $p \in  {R}_{i}$ do

		遍历 $p \in  {R}_{i}$ 执行

			${w}_{i}\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {p.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right) ;$

	$\overrightarrow{w} \leftarrow  \left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle  ;$

	$Z \leftarrow$ index built for $Q,\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ as Lemma 2.1;

	$Z \leftarrow$ 为 $Q,\mathbf{I},\overrightarrow{w}$ 构建索引（如引理2.1）；

	while true do

	循环执行

		$y \leftarrow$ a result enumerated from $Z$ ;

		$y \leftarrow$ 从 $Z$ 枚举出的结果；

		if $y \notin  S$ then break;

		若 $y \notin  S$ 则中断；

	$S \leftarrow  S \cup  \{ y\}$

return $S$ ;

返回 $S$ ；

---

<!-- Media -->

Correctness. For $\overrightarrow{w}$ at line 6 of Algorithm 12, $\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,y}\right)$ for every tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ . Ravi et al. [59],showed that the greedy algorithm returns a $\frac{1}{2}$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary in the non-relational setting. Algorithm 12 implements the greedy algorithm in the relational setting so it also returns a $\frac{1}{2}$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ .

正确性。对于算法12第6行的$\overrightarrow{w}$，每个元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$都满足$\overrightarrow{w}\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i},y.{\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  S}}{\phi }_{H}\left( {t,y}\right)$。Ravi等人[59]证明贪心算法在非关系型场景中返回$\frac{1}{2}$-和多样性$k$-摘要。算法12在关系型场景实现该算法，因此同样会为$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$返回$\frac{1}{2}$-和多样性$k$-摘要。

Complexity. For every tuple $p \in  {R}_{i}$ ,it takes $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ time to compute ${w}_{i}\left( p\right)$ . It takes $O\left( N\right)$ time to construct the index $Z$ . For every tuple $y$ returned by $Z$ ,it takes $O\left( {\log k}\right)  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$ time to check if $y \in  S$ . All tuples can be enumerated from $Z$ within $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ delay. We need to enumerate at most $k - 1$ results until we find one that does not belong in $S$ . Our algorithm runs in $O\left( {{Nk}\log N + {k}^{2}\log N}\right)$ time and uses $O\left( {N + k}\right)$ space.

复杂度。每个元组$p \in  {R}_{i}$计算${w}_{i}\left( p\right)$耗时$O\left( {\log N}\right)$。构建索引$Z$耗时$O\left( N\right)$。对于$Z$返回的每个元组$y$，检查$y \in  S$耗时$O\left( {\log k}\right)  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$。所有元组可在$O\left( {\log N}\right)$延迟内从$Z$枚举完毕。最多枚举$k - 1$个结果即可找到不属于$S$的项。本算法时间复杂度$O\left( {{Nk}\log N + {k}^{2}\log N}\right)$，空间复杂度$O\left( {N + k}\right)$。

Remark. This algorithm cannot be used for the Euclidean metric because the sum of distances from a tuple $t$ to set $S$ is a sum of square roots,so we cannot use the squared Euclidean metric as we did in the algorithms from Section 3 in the ranked enumeration index.

注记。该算法不适用于欧氏度量，因为元组$t$到集合$S$的距离和涉及平方根运算，无法如第3节算法在排序枚举索引中使用平方欧氏度量。

## D Extension to Cyclic Join

## D 循环连接扩展

Generalized Hypertree Decomposition [42]. We need the following notions to extend our results to cyclic joins. We also use a triple(A,E,y)to represent a $\mathrm{{CQ}}\mathcal{Q}$ ,where $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{\mathbf{A}}_{1},{\mathbf{A}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathbf{A}}_{m}}\right\}$ .

广义超树分解[42]。需以下概念将结果扩展至循环连接：用三元组(A,E,y)表示$\mathrm{{CQ}}\mathcal{Q}$，其中$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{\mathbf{A}}_{1},{\mathbf{A}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathbf{A}}_{m}}\right\}$。

Definition D. 1 (Generalized Hypertree Decomposition). Given a join $Q = \left( {\mathrm{A},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a GHD of $Q$ is a pair $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{T}$ is a tree as an ordered set of nodes and $\lambda  : \mathcal{T} \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathrm{A}}$ is a labeling function which associates to each node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ a subset of attributes in $\mathbf{A},{\lambda }_{u}$ ,such that the following conditions are satisfied: (1) For each relation ${\mathbf{A}}_{i} \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,there is a node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ such that ${\mathbf{A}}_{i} \subseteq  {\lambda }_{u}$ ; (2) For each attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ ,the set of nodes $\left\{  {u \in  \mathcal{T} : A \in  {\lambda }_{u}}\right\}$ forms a connected subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ .

定义D.1（广义超树分解）。给定一个连接$Q = \left( {\mathrm{A},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，$Q$的广义超树分解(GHD)是一个二元组$\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$，其中$\mathcal{T}$是作为有序节点集的树，$\lambda  : \mathcal{T} \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathrm{A}}$是标记函数，为每个节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$关联$\mathbf{A},{\lambda }_{u}$中的属性子集，需满足以下条件：(1)对于每个关系${\mathbf{A}}_{i} \in  \mathcal{E}$，存在节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$使得${\mathbf{A}}_{i} \subseteq  {\lambda }_{u}$；(2)对于每个属性$A \in  \mathbf{A}$，节点集$\left\{  {u \in  \mathcal{T} : A \in  {\lambda }_{u}}\right\}$构成$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树。

Definition D.2 (Fractional Edge Covering Number of ${CQ}$ ). Given a $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q = \left( {\mathrm{A},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,a function $W : \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack   \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ is a fractional edge covering of $Q$ if $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack   : A \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}}}W\left( i\right)  \geq  1$ holds for any attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ . The weight of $W$ is defined as $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}W\left( i\right)$ . The fractional edge covering number of $\mathcal{Q}$ is the minimum weight of all possible fractional edge coverings of $Q$ .

定义D.2（${CQ}$的分数边覆盖数）。给定$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q = \left( {\mathrm{A},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，若函数$W : \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack   \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$满足对任意属性$A \in  \mathbf{A}$有$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack   : A \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}}}W\left( i\right)  \geq  1$，则称其为$Q$的分数边覆盖。其权重定义为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}W\left( i\right)$。$\mathcal{Q}$的分数边覆盖数是指$Q$所有可能分数边覆盖的最小权重。

Given a GHD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ for a join $Q$ ,each node $u$ derives a subjoin over attributes ${\lambda }_{u}$ and relations ${\mathcal{E}}_{u} = \{ e \cap  u : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$ . The width of each node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ is defined as the fractional edge covering number of $\left( {{\mathrm{A}}_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right)$ . The width of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ is defined as the maximum width over all nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ . Then, the fractional hypertree width of a join follows:

给定连接$Q$的广义超树分解(GHD)$\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$，每个节点$u$导出基于属性${\lambda }_{u}$和关系${\mathcal{E}}_{u} = \{ e \cap  u : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$的子连接。节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$的宽度定义为$\left( {{\mathrm{A}}_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right)$的分数边覆盖数。$\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$的宽度则是$\mathcal{T}$中所有节点宽度的最大值。由此可得连接的分数超树宽度定义如下：

Definition D. 3 (Fractional Hypertree Width [42]). The fractional hypertree width of a join $Q$ , denoted as fhtw(Q),is fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathcal{T},\lambda \right) }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \mathcal{T}}}\rho \left( {{\lambda }_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right)$ ,i.e.,the minimum width over all GHDs.

定义D.3（分数超树宽度[42]）。连接$Q$的分数超树宽度记作fhtw(Q)，即fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathcal{T},\lambda \right) }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \mathcal{T}}}\rho \left( {{\lambda }_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right)$，表示所有广义超树分解中的最小宽度。

Basically, $O\left( {N}^{\text{fhtw }}\right)$ is an upper bound on the number of join results materialized for each node in $\mathcal{T}$ ,as well as the time complexity of computing the join results [14]. Thus,we can generalize all our results to cyclic joins. If the runtime of an algorithm for the acyclic join was $T\left( {N,k,\varepsilon }\right)$ ,it now becomes $T\left( {{N}^{\text{fhtw }},k,\varepsilon }\right)$ .

本质上，$O\left( {N}^{\text{fhtw }}\right)$是$\mathcal{T}$中每个节点物化连接结果数量的上界，也是计算连接结果时间复杂度[14]的上界。因此我们可以将所有结论推广到循环连接。若算法对无环连接的运行时间为$T\left( {N,k,\varepsilon }\right)$，则现在变为$T\left( {{N}^{\text{fhtw }},k,\varepsilon }\right)$。

## E Extension to join-project queries

## E 连接-投影查询的扩展

As we did for join queries, we focus on acyclic join-project queries and then use the well-known GHD shown in Section 6 and Appendix D that maps any cyclic instance of input size $N$ to an acyclic instance of input size ${N}^{\text{fhtw }}$ . Hence,all our algorithms for acyclic join-project queries can be extended to cyclic join-project queries with the same approximation guarantees. The running time changes from $O\left( {N \cdot  f\left( k\right) }\right)$ ,where $f\left( \cdot \right)$ is a function of $k$ ,to $O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhtw }} \cdot  f\left( k\right) }\right)$ .

如同处理连接查询时一样，我们首先关注无环连接-投影查询，然后利用第6节和附录D中展示的著名广义超树分解(GHD)方法——该方法能将任何输入规模为$N$的循环实例映射为输入规模${N}^{\text{fhtw }}$的无环实例。因此，我们所有针对无环连接-投影查询的算法都能以相同近似保证扩展到循环连接-投影查询。运行时间将从$O\left( {N \cdot  f\left( k\right) }\right)$（其中$f\left( \cdot \right)$是$k$的函数）变为$O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhtw }} \cdot  f\left( k\right) }\right)$。

We first describe the high-level idea. Recall that $y$ is the set of the output attributes. Recall that $d = \left| \mathbf{y}\right|$ . A summary $S$ should be computed with respect to attributes only in $\mathbf{y}$ ,i.e., $S \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ . For a relation ${R}_{i}$ ,let ${R}_{i}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{\mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\left( {R}_{i}\right)$ ,the projection of the tuples in ${R}_{i}$ on the output attributes ${\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime } = \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$ . All our algorithms run almost verbatim using ${R}_{i}^{\prime }$ instead of ${R}_{i}$ . Of course,the original relations ${R}_{i}$ are still used to identify the joined tuples. In fact, all our algorithms are straightforwardly extended to join-project queries if we use a nearest neighbor,farthest neighbor,top- $k$ ,and rectangular oracles that work on join-project queries. Next, we introduce such oracles.

我们首先描述核心思想。请注意$y$是输出属性集合，且$d = \left| \mathbf{y}\right|$。摘要$S$应仅针对$\mathbf{y}$中的属性进行计算，即$S \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$。对于关系${R}_{i}$，令${R}_{i}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{\mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}}\left( {R}_{i}\right)$表示该关系中元组在输出属性${\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime } = \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$上的投影。我们所有算法几乎可以原封不动地使用${R}_{i}^{\prime }$替代${R}_{i}$运行。当然，原始关系${R}_{i}$仍用于识别连接元组。实际上，只要使用能在连接-投影查询上工作的最近邻、最远邻、Top-$k$以及矩形预言器，我们所有算法都能直接扩展到连接-投影查询。接下来我们将介绍这类预言器。

Ranked enumeration. For simplicity,let ${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime } = {\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime } - \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{j < i}}{\mathbf{A}}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$ be the set of active attributes for ${R}_{i}^{\prime }$ i.e.,the set of output attributes that do not appear in any relation before ${R}_{i}^{\prime }$ . Let ${w}_{i} : {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right| } \rightarrow  \mathbb{R}$ be a weight function,which takes as input a tuple $t \in  {R}_{i}$ and outputs a real number. Let $\overrightarrow{w} =$ $\left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$ be a set of weight functions. For a CQ $Q$ ,a database I,and a pair of results ${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ ,we say ${t}_{1}{ \leq  }_{\overrightarrow{w}}{t}_{2}$ if $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{1}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{2}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$ . We use [32] instead of [33] to perform ranked enumeration of join-project queries.

排序枚举。为简化起见，设${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime } = {\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime } - \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{j < i}}{\mathbf{A}}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$是${R}_{i}^{\prime }$的活跃属性集，即那些在${R}_{i}^{\prime }$之前未出现在任何关系中的输出属性。令${w}_{i} : {\mathbb{R}}^{\left| {\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right| } \rightarrow  \mathbb{R}$为权重函数，其输入为元组$t \in  {R}_{i}$并输出实数。设$\overrightarrow{w} =$$\left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$为权重函数集合。对于合取查询(CQ)$Q$、数据库I及结果对${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，当$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{1}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{j}\left( {{t}_{2}.{\mathbf{A}}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$时我们称${t}_{1}{ \leq  }_{\overrightarrow{w}}{t}_{2}$。我们采用文献[32]而非[33]来实现连接-投影查询的排序枚举。

LEMMA E. 1 ([32]). For an acyclic join-project $Q$ ,a database $\mathbf{I}$ ,and a set of weight functions $\overrightarrow{w} =$ $\left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$ ,an index of size $O\left( N\right)$ can be constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time,such that given any value $k \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$ ,the top- $k$ results of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be enumerated in ascending or descending order with respect to $\overrightarrow{w}$ within $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ delay.

引理 E. 1 ([32])。对于无环连接-投影$Q$操作、数据库$\mathbf{I}$以及权重函数集$\overrightarrow{w} =$$\left\langle  {{w}_{1},{w}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{m}}\right\rangle$，可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构建大小为$O\left( N\right)$的索引，使得给定任意值$k \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$时，能够以$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$延迟按$\overrightarrow{w}$升序或降序枚举$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$的前$k$个结果。

We note that the weights in [33] are defined on the attributes rather than on the tuples. However, from our construction, it is straightforward to also design weight functions on the attributes. Hence, for simplicity, we follow the definition of weight functions we had in all previous sections.

我们注意到文献[33]中的权重是定义在属性而非元组上的。不过根据我们的构建方法，直接在属性上设计权重函数也是可行的。因此为简化起见，我们沿用前文各章节中权重函数的定义方式。

Euclidean-based oracles. Let $\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ be a tuple. The nearest neighbor oracle finds a tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ such that $\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$ is minimized. The farthest neighbor oracle finds a tuple $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ such that $\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$ is maximized. For each relation ${R}_{i}$ ,we define ${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$ as: ${w}_{i}\left( {p.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}{\left( p.{A}_{j} - \theta .{A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$ ,where $p \in  {R}_{i}$ . If ${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime } = \varnothing$ then ${w}_{i}\left( {p.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = 0$ . Thanks to the decomposability of squared Euclidean distance,for any query result $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathrm{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}{\left( t.{A}_{j} - \theta .{A}_{j}\right) }^{2} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  \mathbf{y}}}{\left( t.A - \theta .A\right) }^{2} =$ ${\phi }^{2}\left( {\theta ,t}\right)$ ,The square (and square root) function is increasing for non-negative values,so the order of the distances with respect to the squared Euclidean distance is the same as the order of the distances with respect to the Euclidean distance.

基于欧氏距离的预言机。设$\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$为元组，最近邻预言机寻找满足$\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$最小化的元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$，最远邻预言机寻找满足$\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$最大化的元组$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$。对于每个关系${R}_{i}$，我们定义${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$为：${w}_{i}\left( {p.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}{\left( p.{A}_{j} - \theta .{A}_{j}\right) }^{2}$，其中$p \in  {R}_{i}$。若${\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime } = \varnothing$则${w}_{i}\left( {p.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = 0$。得益于平方欧氏距离的可分解性，对于任何查询结果$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) ,\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathrm{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathrm{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}{\left( t.{A}_{j} - \theta .{A}_{j}\right) }^{2} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  \mathbf{y}}}{\left( t.A - \theta .A\right) }^{2} =$${\phi }^{2}\left( {\theta ,t}\right)$，由于平方（及平方根）函数在非负值上单调递增，基于平方欧氏距离的排序结果与基于欧氏距离的排序结果一致。

Top- $k$ oracle. Let $u = \left\langle  {{u}_{1},{u}_{2},\ldots ,{u}_{d}}\right\rangle$ be a vector in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ . The top- $k$ oracle finds the $k$ tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ with the largest inner product with respect to $u$ . For each relation ${R}_{i}$ ,we define ${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$ as: ${w}_{i}\left( {p.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}\left( {p.{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$ ,where $p \in  {R}_{i}$ . It is easy to show that for any query result $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ , $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}\left( {t.{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j} = \langle t,u\rangle .$

Top-$k$预言机。设$u = \left\langle  {{u}_{1},{u}_{2},\ldots ,{u}_{d}}\right\rangle$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中的向量，该预言机可找出$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中与$u$内积最大的$k$个元组。对于每个关系${R}_{i}$，定义${w}_{i}\left( \cdot \right)$为：${w}_{i}\left( {p.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\overline{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}\left( {p.{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j}$，其中$p \in  {R}_{i}$。易证对于任意查询结果$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$，$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}\left( {t.{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\bar{\mathbf{A}}}_{i}^{\prime }}}\left( {t.{A}_{j}}\right)  \cdot  {u}_{j} = \langle t,u\rangle .$

LEMMA E.2. Given an acyclic join-project $Q$ with $d$ output attributes,a database instance $\mathbf{I}$ with input size $N$ ,and a tuple $\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,a set of weight functions $\overrightarrow{w}$ can be constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time,such that the nearest (resp. farthest) neighbor of $\theta$ in $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ ,arg $\mathop{\min }\limits_{{t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }}\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$ (resp. $\arg \mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }}\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$ ), can be computed in $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ time. Similarly,given a vector $u \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,a set of weight functions $\overrightarrow{w}$ can be constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time,such that the $k$ tuples in $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ with the highest inner product with $u$ can be computed in $O\left( {{Nk}\log N}\right)$ time.

引理E.2。给定具有$d$个输出属性的无环连接投影$Q$、输入规模为$N$的数据库实例$\mathbf{I}$及元组$\theta  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构造一组权重函数$\overrightarrow{w}$，使得$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中$\theta$的最近（或最远）邻域arg$\mathop{\min }\limits_{{t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }}\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$（或$\arg \mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right) }}\phi \left( {\theta ,t}\right)$）可在$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$时间内计算。类似地，给定向量$u \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构造权重函数$\overrightarrow{w}$，使得$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中与$u$内积最大的$k$个元组可在$O\left( {{Nk}\log N}\right)$时间内计算。

Rectangular oracle. Similarly to the rectangular oracle in the join queries, we can find all tuples in I that pass the predicate, defined by the rectangle, and then apply the index from [32] to enumerate the query results on the surviving tuples.

矩形预言机。类似于连接查询中的矩形预言机，我们可找出I中满足该矩形谓词的所有元组，随后应用[32]的索引对存活元组枚举查询结果。

LEMMA E.3. Given an acyclic join-project $Q$ with $d$ output attributes,a database instance $\mathbf{I}$ with input size $N$ ,and a rectangle $\psi  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,an index of size $O\left( N\right)$ can be constructed in $O\left( N\right)$ time such that all results in $\psi  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ can be enumerated with $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ delay.

引理E.3。给定具有$d$个输出属性的无环连接投影$Q$、输入规模为$N$的数据库实例$\mathbf{I}$及矩形$\psi  \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内构建大小为$O\left( N\right)$的索引，使得$\psi  \cap  Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$中所有结果能以$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$延迟枚举。

Replacing the oracles we used in the main part with the oracles defined in this section, we conclude with the following results for cohesive and diverse summaries under the Euclidean metric.

将主体部分使用的预言机替换为本节定义的预言机后，我们得出欧几里得度量下聚合性与多样性摘要的如下结论。

COROLLARY E.4. For an acyclic join-project $Q$ of $d$ output attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ , and a parameter $\varepsilon  > 0,a\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$ -cohesive $k$ -summary for $Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ under the Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {{k}^{2}N\log N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time.

推论E.4：对于具有$d$个输出属性的无环连接投影$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，在欧几里得度量下可于$O\left( {{k}^{2}N\log N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$时间内计算出参数为$\varepsilon  > 0,a\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right)$的$k$凝聚型$Q\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$摘要。

COROLLARY E.5. For an acyclic join-project $Q$ of $d$ output attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ , and a parameter $\varepsilon  > 0$ ,a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -min-diverse $k$ -summary of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathbf{I}\right)$ under the Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {{k}^{2}N\log N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$ time.

推论E.5：对于具有$d$个输出属性的无环连接投影$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，在欧几里得度量下可于$O\left( {{k}^{2}N\log N + {kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-d}}\right)$时间内计算出参数为$\varepsilon  > 0$的$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$最小多样性$k$摘要。

COROLLARY E.6. For an acyclic join-project $Q$ of $d$ output attributes,a database $\mathbf{I}$ of input size $N$ , and a parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,\frac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,a $\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$ -sum-diverse $k$ -summary of $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathrm{I}\right)$ under the Euclidean metric can be computed in $O\left( {{kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$ time.

推论E.6：对于具有$d$个输出属性的无环连接投影$Q$，输入规模为$N$的数据库$\mathbf{I}$，在欧几里得度量下可于$O\left( {{kN}\log \left( N\right) {\varepsilon }^{-\left( {d - 1}\right) /2}}\right)$时间内计算出参数为$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,\frac{1}{2}}\right)$的$\left( {\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon }\right)$求和多样性$k$摘要。

Equivalently, using the oracles defined in this section, we can derive the results for constructing cohesive and diverse summaries under the Hamming metric.

等效地，利用本节定义的预言机，可推导出汉明度量下构建凝聚型与多样性摘要的结果。
